Conclusion: 启发式分离权重$w_1\gg w_2\gg\cdots\gg w_L$回答了错误的问题。精确等价需要单位性能切片中的权重，且该权重只能保证逐点有效。因此实际应用应采用受监控的加权求解加选择性级联回退策略，不过合规模式监控器只能检测到部分实际失效情况。

Abstract: Automated-driving rulebooks rank rule violations lexicographically, and model predictive control enforces that ranking either exactly, through $L{+}1$ sequential programs per tick, or approximately, through a weighted sum tuned by the separation heuristic $w_1\gg w_2\gg\cdots\gg w_L$. We show the heuristic answers the wrong question. For a convex priority-ordered program, a weighted sum reproduces the lexicographic optimum precisely when its weight, augmented by a unit performance coefficient, supports the upper image of the achievement map at the lexicographic point; the admissible weights form the unit-performance slice of an outward normal cone. Under hinge penalties this slice is a polyhedron obtained by projecting a scaled-KKT system, and a linear program returns an interior weight with a certified margin; under squared-hinge penalties no finite weight is exact whenever the limiting multiplier is nonzero, with violation along the local minimizer branch decaying as $O(1/w)$. Calibrated on held-out logs, the resulting weights have near-equal tier components in nine of eleven calibration-eligible scenario classes and roughly double legal-tier event precision against a matched heuristic weight in closed-loop nuPlan experiments on a 25-rule rulebook. The certificate is, however, pointwise: no single weight is valid across the sampled ticks of an episode, the median lifetime is one sampling interval (zero subsequent ticks at the native rate), and persistence tracks active-set stability. These findings motivate monitored weighted solves with selective cascade fallback, although the compliance-pattern monitor detects only a subset of measured lapses.

</details>


### [94] [Optimal Heat Storage Sizing for District Heating Networks to Maximize Electricity Revenue from Combined Heat and Power Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12525)
*Martin Sollich,Maarten Blommaert*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的自动化数学优化方法，用于确定区域供热网络（DHN）中短期储热的最优规模。该方法同时建模DHN、热生产者与储热系统，以最小化总经济成本。在比利时一个第三代CHP-DHN（30个消费者）上验证，最优储热集成使20年成本降低73万欧元（16.5%），并展示了相比简化方法在可行设计、成本评估准确性和最优储热规模确定方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管储热在DHN中的效益已得到充分验证，但由于DHN在热源、生产与消费模式、网络热损失及燃料成本方面的高度多样性，确定最优储热规模仍然具有挑战性。现有基于物理的方法规模有限，而简化方法难以准确识别最优储热规模。

Method: 采用非线性、基于物理的数学优化方法，同时对区域供热网络、热生产者与储热系统进行建模。通过显式考虑随时间变化的热生产成本、热需求与热损失，以最小化总经济成本为目标进行最优储热规模优化，该方法是可扩展且自动化的。

Result: 在比利时一个拥有30个消费者的第三代CHP-DHN上验证，超过以往基于物理的储热规模研究规模。最优储热集成将20年成本从441万欧元降至368万欧元，节省73万欧元（16.5%）。节省主要来自热生产成本降低107万欧元（通过将热提取转移到电价低的时段），储热投资成本为32.3万欧元。与常用简化储热规模方法对比，后者无法识别最优储热规模，证明了所提方法的优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的整体性、基于物理的优化方法能够确保可行设计、准确成本评估与最优储热规模确定，优于常用的简化储热规模方法，为多样化的DHN系统提供了一种可扩展的储热优化解决方案。

Abstract: Integrating heat storages in district heating networks (DHNs) supports managing dynamic characteristics such as heat-demand variations and changing energy prices, and the intermittency of renewable sources. A common application are DHNs with combined heat and power (CHP) units, where a storage allows shifting heat extraction to periods with favorable electricity prices. Although the benefits of heat storage in DHNs are well established, determining the optimal storage size remains challenging due to the diversity of DHNs in terms of heat sources, production and consumption patterns, network heat losses, and fuel costs. This paper presents a scalable, automated methodology for optimizing short-term heat storage in DHNs using mathematical optimization. The nonlinear, physics-based approach models the DHN, heat producers, and storages simultaneously to minimize total economic cost through optimal storage sizing, explicitly considering time-varying heat-production costs, heat demands, and heat losses. The methodology is demonstrated on a 3rd-generation CHP-DHN in Belgium with 30 consumers, exceeding the scale of previous physics-based storage-sizing studies. For this system, optimal storage integration reduces the 20-year cost by 730 k EUR (16.5%), from 4.41 M EUR to 3.68 M EUR. The reduction results from a 1.07 M EUR decrease in heat-production cost achieved by shifting heat extraction to periods of low electricity prices, while the storage investment amounts to 323 k EUR. A comparison to a commonly used simplified storage sizing method, which fails to identify the optimal storage size, demonstrates the advantage of the proposed holistic, physics-based optimization approach in ensuring feasible designs, accurate cost assessments, and optimal storage sizing.

</details>


### [95] [A Local-Linearly Convergent Algorithm for Nonconvex Equality-Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12665)
*Frank E. Curtis,Lingjun Guo,Daniel P. Robinson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Gradient-Eigenstep算法的收敛性分析，证明其在靠近强二阶稳定点初始化时可获得局部线性收敛速率，并展示其可作为渐进采样策略中的高效子问题求解器，改进大规模样本平均形式等式约束优化问题的最坏样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸等式约束优化问题在实际应用中广泛存在，但现有的求解方法或迭代效率不高，或样本复杂度不佳。Gradient-Eigenstep算法虽然是一种迭代高效的方法，但其收敛速率分析和在样本平均场景下的应用尚未充分研究。

Method: 论文从两个方面进行理论分析：(1) 证明当算法从强二阶稳定点附近初始化，并选取足够小的步长参数和足够大的罚参数时，算法退化为对Fletcher增广拉格朗日函数的梯度下降，获得局部线性收敛速率；(2) 将上述结果应用于渐进采样（progressive sampling）策略，将Gradient-Eigenstep算法作为子问题求解器，分析其最坏情况样本复杂度。

Result: 获得了两项主要结果：一是Gradient-Eigenstep算法在特定条件下具有局部线性收敛速率；二是将其嵌入渐进采样框架后，与直接求解全样本问题相比，获得了改进的最坏情况样本复杂度。

Conclusion: Gradient-Eigenstep算法不仅本身具有良好的局部收敛特性，还能作为渐进采样策略中的高效子问题求解器，为大样本平等式约束优化问题提供样本复杂度更优的求解方案。

Abstract: For solving nonconvex equality-constrained optimization problems, a recent Gradient-Eigenstep Algorithm by Goyens et al.~is an iteration-efficient approach, based on minimizing Fletcher's augmented Lagrangian function, for finding an approximate second-order stationary point from an arbitrary starting point. In this paper, the analysis of this algorithm is extended, offering a two-fold contribution. First, it is shown that a local-linear rate of convergence can be obtained by this method if it is initiated sufficiently close to a strong second-order stationary point and employs a sufficiently small step-size parameter and sufficiently large penalty parameter. In this case, the algorithm reduces to a gradient descent algorithm applied to minimize Fletcher's augmented Lagrangian. Second, as a particularly useful application of the first result, it is shown that the Gradient-Eigenstep algorithm can be used as an iteration-efficient subproblem solver in the context of a progressive sampling strategy for solving equality-constrained optimization problems when the objective and constraint functions are defined by large sample averages, ultimately offering an algorithm with an improved worst-case sample complexity when compared to an approach that solves a full-sample problem directly.

</details>


### [96] [Stochastic Mixed-Integer Optimization of Dynamic Electricity Tariffs with Consumer Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12688)
*Ananya Kale,Mohit Apte,Chhaya Gosavi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在保护消费者账单（设定收入与账单增幅上限）的前提下，住宅日前电价优化的削峰能力损失。基于5,566户伦敦低碳家庭的半小时数据，通过准实验估计价格响应、bootstrap生成需求情景，并求解随机混合整数规划。结果表明：分段保护的随机电价平均削峰2.29%，收入变化-2.26%，最差分段账单增幅2.48%；移除分段账单上限仅将削峰提升至2.37%，但最差分段账单增幅升至6.81%。分段平均上限无法约束住户尾部风险（p95账单增幅8.61%），改用代表性住户（尾部感知）变体可将p95降至7.05%而几乎不损失平均削峰性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日前住宅电价必须在需求观测前公布，激进的定价虽在模拟中能削峰，但可能大幅抬高用户账单。因此需要衡量在要求收入接近平电费基线和限制账单增幅的约束下，峰削减会损失多少，即量化"价格保护"（价格优化的公平性约束）的代价。

Method: 使用5,566户Low Carbon London家庭的半小时数据（1.678亿条有效读数），以标准费率组为对照组进行准实验价格响应估计；通过bootstrap生成需求场景；构建并求解随机混合整数规划（使用HiGHS求解器）；比较分段保护与代表性住户（尾部感知）两种账单保护策略。

Result: 73个合格留出测试日上，分段保护随机电价平均削峰2.29%（95%日bootstrap CI [2.11, 2.48]），收入变化-2.26%，最差分段账单增幅2.48%；移除分段上限后削峰仅升至2.37%，但最差分段账单增幅升至6.81%。分段平均上限未约束住户尾部（p95=8.61%，CVaR95=14.76%）；尾部感知变体将p95降至7.05%，平均削峰仅从2.29%变至2.28%。

Conclusion: 在此样本中，显著的平均账单保护仅损失极少削峰性能，但分段平均上限无法保护住户尾部分布；尾部感知的代表性住户保护能在几乎不损失削峰性能的前提下显著降低住户尾部账单增幅。所有优化结果均为基于模型的反事实（在opt-in试验条件下），且因缺乏批发成本数据而不优化利润。

Abstract: Day-ahead residential tariffs must be posted before demand is observed. Aggressive high prices can cut peaks in simulation, but they can also raise customer bills. This paper measures how much peak reduction is lost when a tariff design problem is required to keep revenue near a flat-tariff baseline and to limit bill increases. Using half-hourly data from 5,566 Low Carbon London households (167.8 million validated readings), we estimate quasi-experimental price response against the standard-tariff comparison group, form bootstrap demand scenarios, and solve stochastic mixed-integer programs with HiGHS. On all 73 eligible held-out test days, a segment-protected stochastic tariff reduces simulated peak demand by 2.29% on average (95% day-bootstrap CI [2.11, 2.48]), with revenue change -2.26% and mean worst-segment bill increase 2.48%. Removing the segment bill cap raises peak reduction only to 2.37%, while the worst segment bill increase rises to 6.81%: in this sample, substantial average protection costs little peak-shaving performance. The same schedules leave a household 95th-percentile bill increase of 8.61% (CVaR95 14.76%), so segment-average caps do not bound household tails. We report the full price-of-protection frontier under consistent household simulation and compare segment protection with a representative-household (tail-aware) variant: the latter cuts household p95 from 8.61% to 7.05% while changing mean peak reduction only from 2.29% to 2.28%. All optimized outcomes are model-based counterfactuals under an opt-in trial; wholesale costs are unavailable, so we do not optimize profit.

</details>


### [97] [An Algebraic Method for Optimizing the State, Control, and Terminal State Weight Matrices for Optimal Feedback Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12702)
*Daegyun Choi,Donghoon Kim,James D. Turner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用闭式解生成最优反馈控制算法权值矩阵的优化方法。传统上，权值矩阵（状态/控制/终端惩罚矩阵）的选择依赖实验性迭代，且优化过程需要多次昂贵的数值积分。作者通过时变Riccati矩阵、状态轨迹、状态转移矩阵及最优性能指标的闭式解，以解析方式计算所有偏导数，避免了数值积分，并利用解析梯度进行优化，以最小化终端状态值。数值算例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优反馈控制算法的性能指标中存在自由参数（状态/控制/终端惩罚权值矩阵），其选取过程通常是实验性和迭代性的。现有优化方法需要多次数值积分，计算开销巨大，亟需一种更高效的计算方案。

Method: 利用时变Riccati矩阵、状态轨迹、状态转移矩阵及最优性能指标的闭式代数解，以解析方式生成所有偏导数，从而构造优化步骤所需的解析梯度；优化策略以最小化反馈控制问题的终端状态值为目标，全程无需数值积分。

Result: 通过数值算例验证了所提出优化算法的有效性，证明其能够以更低的计算成本完成权值矩阵的优化选取。

Conclusion: 所提出的闭式解析梯度优化方法显著降低了权值矩阵选取的计算开销，预期可广泛应用于科学与工程领域的控制理论应用中。

Abstract: The necessary conditions for formulating optimal feedback control algorithms have been known for many years. Free parameters exist in the performance index in the form of state and control penalty and terminal state penalty matrices for tuning the performance of the optimally controlled system. The selection process is typically experimental and iterative. To generate the weight matrices for optimal feedback control algorithms, an optimization process is proposed. Typically, the optimization process for the weight matrices requires several numerical integration processes that are computationally expensive; this work overcomes the classical high computational cost by exploiting closed-form solutions for the time-varying Riccati matrix, state trajectories, state transition matrix, and the optimal performance index. Closed-form algebraic equations are used to generate all partial derivative calculations, and no numerical integration is required. The closed-form partial derivatives are used to generate analytic gradients for the optimization steps. The optimization strategy seeks to minimize the terminal state values for the feedback control problem. A numerical example is presented to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed optimization algorithm. The resulting computational procedures are expected to be broadly useful for control theory applications in science and engineering.

</details>


### [98] [Efficient Hessian-Free Methods for Multi-Objective Bilevel Optimization with Nonconvex Lower Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12704)
*Yicong Jiang,Feihu Huang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出MOMEHA及其随机变体MB-MOMEHA，首次解决下层级非凸的多目标双层优化问题，利用Moreau包络将原问题转化为带包络约束的单层优化，并通过平滑加权Tchebycheff标量化实现单循环且免Hessian计算的算法，在理论上证明了确定性和随机设定下的收敛性，在少样本元学习和神经架构搜索实验上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标双层优化在AI领域（如自动学习和多任务元学习）有广泛应用，但现有方法依赖于下层级为（强）凸的假设。然而实际中的多目标双层学习问题通常是非凸的，特别是下层级问题本质非凸，现有方法无法处理此类场景，存在明显的理论和方法空白。

Method: 1) 利用Moreau包络将原始多目标双层问题转化为带包络约束的多目标单层优化问题；2) 引入平滑加权Tchebycheff标量化，使算法在多目标设定下保持单循环（single-loop）且免Hessian（Hessian-free）的计算优势；3) 提出基于动量（momentum）的随机变体MB-MOMEHA，用于处理随机多目标双层学习问题。

Result: 在理论上，提供了所提算法在确定性和随机两种设定下的收敛性保证；在实验上，通过在少样本元学习和神经架构搜索任务中的验证，表明该方法在Pareto前沿上优于现有方法，具有有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文填补了多目标双层优化在下层级非凸场景下的空白，MOMEHA和MB-MOMEHA在理论上具备收敛保证，在实验上超越现有方法，为多目标双层非凸学习问题提供了高效且实用的求解框架。

Abstract: Multi-objective bilevel optimization has wide applications in the AI area such as automated learning and multi-task meta-learning. Although recently some works have been begun to study the multi-objective bilevel optimization, the proposed methods rely on the (strongly) convex lower level problems. In fact, these multi-objective bilevel learning problems are generally nonconvex, and particularly their lower level problems are nonconvex. To fill this gap, we propose a class of Multi-Objective Moreau Envelope based Hessian-free Algorithms (MOMEHA) to solve the multi-objective bilevel learning problems with nonconvex lower level. Specifically, our method uses the Moreau envelope to convert the original problem into a multi-objective single-level optimization with an envelope constraint. In particular, our method retains computational advantages of being single-loop and Hessian-free in the multi-objective setting by incorporating a smooth weighted Tchebycheff scalarization. Furthermore, we propose a momentum-based variant of MOMEHA (i.e., MB-MOMEHA) method to solve the stochastic multi-objective bilevel learning problems. In theory, we provide the convergence properties of our algorithms under both deterministic and stochastic setting. Some experiments on few-shot meta-learning and neural architecture search demonstrate that our methods outperform the existing approaches in Pareto front, validating its effectiveness and robustness.

</details>


### [99] [Shape Design for Degenerate Hyperbolic Equation with Degenerate Boundary and Its Application to Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12754)
*Dong-Hui Yang,Yuanzhi Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类退化双曲型方程的可观测性与可控性，其中控制区域与退化集相交。通过引入基于形状设计的逼近方法，将退化方程近似为一族一致双曲方程，并证明可观测性不等式在逼近参数下一致成立，最终通过退化极限得到原始退化方程的可观测性不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于退化双曲方程的研究主要处理控制区域与退化集分离的情形，而对控制区域触及退化部分的情形缺乏有效方法。退化性带来的数学困难使得传统的估计方法失效，需要新的技术路径来处理这一更具挑战性的设定。

Method: 本文提出一种基于形状设计的逼近方法：用一族一致双曲方程逼近退化方程。证明过程依赖于三个关键技术：(1) 相关算子的谱逼近；(2) 弱解的精细估计；(3) 乘子方法。首先对逼近方程建立常数与逼近参数无关的可观测性不等式，再通过退化极限过渡到原始退化方程。

Result: 成功建立了退化双曲方程在控制区域与退化集相交情形下的可观测性不等式，并由此推导出相应的可控性结论。

Conclusion: 本文首次解决了控制区域穿越退化集的双曲方程可观测性与可控性问题，为更广泛退化PDE的能控性理论提供了新方法框架，拓展了该领域的边界。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the observability and controllability of a class of degenerate hyperbolic equations with a control region intersecting the degenerate set. Unlike the existing results that mainly deal with control regions separated from the degeneracy, we consider the case where the control region reaches the degenerate part. To handle the difficulty caused by the degeneracy, we introduce a shape-design-based approximation method based on shape design by approximating the degenerate equation with a family of uniformly hyperbolic equations. The proof relies on the spectral approximation of the associated operators, precise estimates for weak solutions, and the multiplier method. We first establish observability inequalities for the approximating equations with constants independent of the approximation parameter. Then, by passing to the degenerate limit, we obtain the observability inequality for the original degenerate equation.

</details>


### [100] [Difference-of-Convex Regularization for Graph Learning by Differentiable Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12757)
*Liping Tao,Chee Wei Tan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种差凸正则化器（DCR）图学习框架，通过正则化最大似然估计近似图拉普拉斯伪逆的谱作用，无需直接求逆，从而解决拉普拉斯伪逆稠密且病态的计算难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉普拉斯正则化最小化是信号处理与机器学习中的基础方法，但受限于图拉普拉斯伪逆的稠密性和病态性。伪逆计算直接进行代价高昂，且伪逆学习比拉普拉斯学习更具挑战性，亟需一种避免直接求逆的近似方法。

Method: 在给定图拉普拉斯的条件下，提出基于差凸正则化器（DCR）的图学习框架，通过对偶表示重写拉普拉斯正则化非负最小二乘（LR-NNLS），将伪逆学习与实例特定推断解耦；采用可微对偶引导学习方案高效重建原始解，并通过正则化最大似然估计（MLE）近似拉普拉斯伪逆的谱作用。

Result: 数值实验表明，DCR相比凸求解器和图滤波基线在性能上有所提升，且在不同图拓扑下具有鲁棒性。理论上证明了DCR算法的稳定性和唯一不动点的存在性。

Conclusion: DCR框架有效避免了直接计算拉普拉斯伪逆的瓶颈，在不求逆的情况下近似其谱作用，兼顾了理论保证与实证性能，为大规模拉普拉斯正则化问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Laplacian-regularized minimization is fundamental in signal processing and machine learning, but is limited by the dense and ill-conditioned nature of the graph Laplacian pseudoinverse. While the Laplacian itself is sparse, its pseudoinverse is dense and often ill-conditioned, rendering direct computation impractical at scale. Moreover, pseudoinverse learning is more challenging than Laplacian learning. To address this challenge, this paper considers the setting where the graph Laplacian is given and proposes a Difference-of-Convex Regularizer (DCR) graph learning framework that approximates the spectral action of the Laplacian pseudoinverse without direct inversion via regularized Maximum Likelihood Estimation (MLE). By reformulating Laplacian-Regularized Nonnegative Least Squares (LR-NNLS) through a dual representation, DCR decouples pseudoinverse learning from instance-specific inference and enables efficient primal solution reconstruction via a differentiable dual-guided learning scheme. We establish theoretical guarantees on stability and the existence of a unique fixed point for DCR algorithm. Numerical experiments demonstrate improved performance over convex solvers and graph filtering baselines and robust performance across diverse graph topologies.

</details>


### [101] [A penalty-type method for relaxed inverse optimal control problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12769)
*Floriane Mefo Kue,Patrick Mehlitz,Thorsten Raasch*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种惩罚型方法，用于数值求解一类源于逆最优控制的双层优化问题。该方法通过求解一系列替代问题的平稳点来逼近松弛值函数重构的平稳点，无需线搜索，每轮迭代仅需评估一次下层问题，且罚参数无需趋于无穷大，在合理假设下获得了子序列收敛结果，并通过数值实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆最优控制问题通过数值手段求解时面临着双重困难：一是该类双层优化问题具有复杂的约束结构（松弛值函数约束），直接求解困难；二是传统方法通常需要线搜索、多次下层问题评估或令罚参数趋于无穷大，计算代价高且不稳定。因此需要设计一种高效且理论上有保障的数值方法。

Method: 提出一种惩罚型方法：(1) 将松弛值函数重构中的约束进行惩罚，构造一系列替代问题；(2) 上层与下层决策变量的更新在每一步中被解耦（decoupled）；(3) 罚参数仅在迭代未带来充分可行性改进时才放大，而非单调趋向无穷大；(4) 算法不包含任何线搜索过程，下层问题每轮仅评估一次，极大降低了计算成本。

Result: 在合理的假设条件下，该方法获得了子序列收敛性结果；数值实验中同时将松弛参数驱向零，实验结果验证了所提方法在实际求解中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法为逆最优控制相关的双层优化问题提供了一种高效且理论扎实的数值求解方案，其核心优势在于避免了线搜索、减少了下层问题评估次数且无需将罚参数推向无穷，同时仍能保证收敛性质。

Abstract: This paper is devoted to the introduction and analysis
  of a penalty-type method for the numerical treatment of
  a class of bilevel optimization problems arising from inverse optimal control.
  The algorithm is designed to compute stationary points of the associated relaxed value function reformulation.
  This is achieved by determining a sequence of stationary points associated with a sequence of surrogate problems
  where the relaxed value function constraint is penalized,
  where the updates of upper- and lower-level decision variables are decoupled,
  and where the penalty parameter is enlarged only in those iterations
  which do not come along with a sufficient improvement of some feasibility measure.
  The resulting method does not comprise any linesearch, the lower-level problem has to be evaluated just once per iteration,
  and the penalty parameter does not need to be driven to infinity.
  Nevertheless, subsequential convergence results are obtained under reasonable assumptions.
  Numerical experiments, where the relaxation parameter is also driven to zero, visualize effectiveness of the approach.

</details>


### [102] [Adaptive Schauder Stochastic Mirror Descent in Banach Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13121)
*Jinhui Bai,Shuai Lu,Lei Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文首次将随机镜像下降（SMD）算法从有限维推广到无限维Banach空间，用于求解仅能通过采样获取随机梯度信息的风险泛函最小化问题。通过构造基于熵函数的Bregman距离、利用Schauder基引入自适应有限维子空间及新的正则化策略，使SMD子问题可显式求解，在样本量为n时实现O(n^{1+θ})时间和O(n^θ)内存的复杂度，并证明了O(n^{-1/p_1})的收敛率（至对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机镜像下降算法主要针对有限维欧几里得空间或有限维Banach空间设计，而在无限维Banach空间中处理风险最小化问题（特别是仅能通过采样获得随机梯度信息的情形）尚缺乏系统的理论框架。此外，在无限维空间中，Bregman距离的构造、子问题的可解性以及计算复杂性都是重大挑战，亟需一种既能保证理论收敛性又能高效计算的算法。

Method: 本文首先根据Banach空间的均匀凸性（uniform convexity）选择Bregman距离；对非均匀凸空间L^1_{ρ_X}(Ω)，构造由熵函数诱导的Bregman距离。基于Banach空间的Schauder基，引入一组随样本量n自适应调整的有限维子空间族。每次SMD迭代中，将子问题限制在相应有限维子空间上，并将随机梯度投影到对应的有限维对偶空间，从而引入新的正则化策略使得子问题可显式求解。同时发展了一套新的分析框架来证明算法的收敛性。

Result: 算法在计算复杂度上达到O(n^{1+θ})时间和O(n^θ)内存（θ>0在最小元具有足够正则性时可取任意小）。证明了所提算法达到O(n^{-1/p_1})的收敛率（至对数因子），其中p_1≥2由底空间凸性性质决定。在设定错误（misspecified）情境下，即最小元仅满足较弱正则条件时，算法仍能收敛到最小值。此外，算法被进一步扩展到反问题（inverse problems）中，并通过数值实验验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为无限维Banach空间中的风险最小化问题提供了一种高效的随机镜像下降算法，在理论上保证了收敛率，在计算上实现了近线性的复杂度，并扩展到了反问题领域。该工作填补了无限维随机优化算法的空白，为后续研究提供了新的分析框架和正则化策略。

Abstract: In this paper, we extend stochastic mirror descent (SMD) to infinite-dimensional Banach spaces for solving a class of risk functional minimization problems, where stochastic gradient information is only available through sampling. We first choose the Bregman distance according to the uniform convexity properties of the Banach space. For the non-uniformly convex space $\mathcal{L}_{ρ_X}^1(Ω)$, we instead construct a Bregman distance induced by the entropy function. Based on a Schauder basis of the Banach space, we introduce a family of finite-dimensional subspaces that adapt to the sample size $n$. At each SMD iteration, we restrict the subproblem to the corresponding finite-dimensional subspace and project the stochastic gradient onto the associated finite-dimensional dual space, thereby introducing a new regularization strategy. This regularization strategy allows us to explicitly solve the SMD subproblem efficiently and to achieve a bias--variance trade-off. The algorithm requires $\mathcal{O}(n^{1+θ})$ time and $\mathcal{O}(n^θ)$ memory, where $θ>0$ can be chosen arbitrarily small when the minimizer has sufficient regularity. By developing a new analytical framework, we prove that the proposed algorithm achieves a convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}\left(n^{-1/p_1}\right)$ up to logarithmic factors, where $p_1\geq2$ is determined by the convexity properties of the underlying space. In the misspecified setting where the minimizer satisfies only weaker regularity conditions, we show that the proposed algorithm still converges to the minimum. We further extend the algorithm to inverse problems and validate its effectiveness in numerical experiments.

</details>


### [103] [Mean Field Stackelberg Game for Production and Carbon Emission Reduction with State Reflections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12775)
*Lijun Bo,Zhen Liu,Jingfei Wang,Jiacheng Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个带控制平均场交互与共同噪声的两层Stackelberg博弈模型，用于分析全球变暖背景下中央监管者与多异构地区之间的减排博弈，并通过状态反射机制实施硬排放上限，建立了近似Stackelberg均衡的存在性并进行了蒙特卡洛敏感性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球变暖由人为碳排放驱动，具有跨国界污染特征和不可逆损害，对人类构成生存威胁。需要一种能整合层级决策、体现排放动态且能实施硬性排放约束的博弈模型，以模拟中央监管者与多地区之间的策略互动。

Method: 构建一个带控制平均场交互（mean field interaction of controls）和共同噪声（common noise）的两层Stackelberg博弈模型。领导者（中央监管者）通过调整产品价格引导n个异构竞争地区（跟随者），并通过反射机制（local time process）在状态反射排放动态下实施硬排放上限。建立了近似Stackelberg均衡的存在性定理。

Result: 成功建立了该博弈模型下近似Stackelberg均衡的存在性，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟进行了敏感性分析，验证了模型行为。

Conclusion: 该研究为全球碳排放治理中的层级决策问题提供了理论框架，通过Stackelberg博弈与平均场理论的结合，模拟了监管者定价引导与硬性排放约束并存下的多主体博弈均衡及其性质。

Abstract: Global warming, driven by anthropogenic carbon emissions with transboundary pollution characteristics and irreversible damage, poses an existential threat to human society. This paper develops a novel two-level Stackelberg game with mean field interaction of controls and common noise which integrates hierarchical decision-making under a state-reflected emission dynamics. A central regulator (leader) adjusts product prices to guide $n$ heterogeneous competing regions (followers) while enforcing a hard emission cap via a reflection mechanism that models emergency reductions through a local time process. We establish the existence of an approximate Stackelberg equilibrium and perform sensitivity analysis via Monte Carlo simulations.

</details>


### [104] [Feedback Diagonalization and Stabilization for Discrete Linear Ensemble Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12813)
*Jonathan M. Bosnich,Xudong Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个由可数无穷多个线性标量控制系统组成的离散线性集成系统，所有系统共享一个公共标量控制输入。文章提供了存在静态线性反馈控制律，使闭环集成系统同时满足极点位于闭左半平面且无穷小生成元可对角化的充要条件，从而保证系统稳定。结果表明，序列(|b_n|)和(a_n/|b_n|)的指数衰减是必要的，并且在期望极点满足一定假设下也是充分的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与有限维线性系统不同，无穷维线性系统仅当极点位于开左半平面时并不能保证稳定性；稳定性需要满足(i)极点位于闭左半平面且(ii)无穷小生成元可对角化。本文旨在为离散线性集成系统提供一个公共反馈控制律，使其闭环系统满足这两个条件，从而确保整体稳定性。

Method: 本文从无穷维线性系统理论出发，考虑由参数对(a_n,b_n)（a_n>0）描述的无穷多个线性标量系统，它们共享一个公共标量控制输入。作者分析了一个静态线性反馈控制律下闭环系统的谱性质与生成元的可对角化性，通过推导必要条件与充分条件，给出了系统可反馈镇定的完整刻画。

Result: 证明了存在静态线性反馈控制律使得闭环集成系统稳定（即满足极点位于闭左半平面且生成元可对角化）的充要条件：序列(|b_n|)和(a_n/|b_n|)的指数衰减是必要的；在关于期望极点的适当假设下，该条件也是充分的。

Conclusion: 本文为离散线性集成系统的反馈镇定提供了严格的理论条件，揭示了系统参数序列的指数衰减特性在保证无穷维系统稳定性中的核心作用，为相关控制设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We consider a countably infinite collection of linear, scalar control systems, where each system is represented by the pair $(a_n,b_n)$ with $a_n>0$ for $n\in \mathbb{N}$, and all systems are forced by a common scalar control input. We refer to this collection of systems as a discrete linear ensemble system. The ensemble system is feedback stabilizable if there exists a common feedback control input that asymptotically stabilizes every system simultaneously. Unlike finite-dimensional linear systems, an infinite-dimensional linear system is not guaranteed to be stable if its poles lie in the open left half-plane. Stability is guaranteed, however, if (i) its poles are contained in the closed left half-plane and (ii) its infinitesimal generator is diagonalizable. In this paper, we provide necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of a static, linear feedback control law that ensures the closed-loop ensemble system satisfies (i) and (ii). In particular, we show that the exponential decay of the sequences $(|b_n|)_{n\in \mathbb{N}}$ and $(a_n/|b_n|)_{n\in \mathbb{N}}$ is necessary and, under an assumption on the desired poles, sufficient.

</details>


### [105] [A Lebeau--Robbiano approach to the controllability of the Boussinesq system with a reduced number of controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12815)
*Víctor Hernández-Santamaría*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明二维和三维Boussinesq系统在减少控制数量（温度方程和仅N-2个速度分量）下的局部零能控性，基于Lebeau-Robbiano谱方法，通过混合可观测性估计克服Stokes算子和Dirichlet Laplacian谱分解不一致的困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Boussinesq系统描述了流体在热耦合下的运动，其控制问题具有重要的理论与应用价值。传统上对耦合系统进行能控性分析需要较多的控制输入，而在实际应用中减少控制数量是一个关键需求。该系统的核心难点在于速度方程和温度方程分别由Stokes算子和Dirichlet Laplacian支配，二者的谱分解结构不同，无法直接应用常规的分量逐项谱论证方法。

Method: 采用Lebeau-Robbiano方法的谱途径。关键创新在于：(1) 利用线性化系统的级联结构克服耦合困难；(2) 建立混合可观测性估计，其中仅对Stokes分量进行谱局部化，而热（温度）分量不做任何频率限制处理；(3) 结合高Stokes频率的耗散性进行Lebeau-Robbiano迭代；(4) 通过时间迭代论证将线性能控性估计转移到非线性Boussinesq系统。

Result: 成功证明二维和三维Boussinesq系统在减少数量控制下的局部零能控性，即控制仅作用于温度方程和N-2个速度分量。控制的小时间代价为C exp(C/T)阶，恢复了预期的时间（抛物型）阶，同时保持了减少的控制数量。

Conclusion: 本文提出的混合可观测性估计和级联结构分析方法，为处理具有不同谱分解结构的耦合偏微分方程系统的能控性问题提供了新思路。控制代价C exp(C/T)表明该结果在时间依赖上具有抛物型最优阶，而减少控制数量的能力增强了该结果的实用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove the local null controllability of the Boussinesq system in dimensions two and three with a reduced number of localized controls. More precisely, the controls act on the temperature equation and on only $N-2$ components of the velocity equation. Our proof is based on a spectral approach in the spirit of the Lebeau--Robbiano method.
  The main difficulty comes from the coupled structure of the system. The velocity and temperature equations are governed by the Stokes operator and the Dirichlet Laplacian, respectively. Their natural spectral decompositions are different, and there is no common spectral localization naturally adapted to the coupled system. Therefore, the usual componentwise spectral argument cannot be applied directly. We show that the cascade structure of the linearized system makes it possible to overcome this difficulty. The main ingredient is a mixed observability estimate in which only the Stokes component is spectrally localized, while the heat component is treated without any frequency restriction. Combining this estimate with the dissipation of the high Stokes frequencies allows us to carry out a Lebeau--Robbiano iteration for the linearized system. The resulting linear controllability estimate is transferred to the nonlinear Boussinesq system through a time-iteration argument. As an important consequence, the controls have a small-time cost bounded by $C\exp(C/T)$, which recovers the expected parabolic order while preserving the reduced number of controls.

</details>


### [106] [Distribution Steering via Sliced Optimal Transport Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12828)
*Kaito Ito,Anqi Dong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于切片最优传输的有限时域分布控制框架。在每个采样时刻，通过投影最优传输映射构造方向性终端条件，其最小能量实现产生随机单方向控制器；对所有投影方向取平均得到确定性切片反馈。对于单积分器动力学，该平均反馈使切片Wasserstein距离单调非增；对于高斯端点分布，反馈是仿射的并保持高斯性，能将均值和协方差驱动至预定值。作者还识别出依赖分布的增益，可在线性衰减率下刻画控制能量，并证明了随机控制器在采样周期趋零时收敛到平均切片流。该构造进一步推广到线性动力系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优传输理论为分布驱动问题提供了自然的几何框架，但其实现通常需要在全状态空间中构造传输映射或耦合，计算代价高。切片最优传输通过一维投影避免了全维构造，但其产生的投影映射只给出方向性位移，不能直接构成可实现的反馈控制律。本文旨在弥合切片最优传输与控制理论之间的鸿沟，设计实际可实现的有限时域反馈控制框架。

Method: 方法包含以下核心步骤：(1) 在每一采样时刻，利用切片最优传输的投影映射定义方向性终端条件；(2) 通过最小能量实现将该条件转化为随机化的单方向控制器；(3) 对所有投影方向取平均，得到确定性的切片反馈控制律；(4) 针对单积分器动力学证明切片Wasserstein距离的单调性，对高斯端点分布证明反馈的仿射性和高斯保持性；(5) 识别依赖分布的增益以实现线性衰减并显式刻画控制能量；(6) 证明随机控制器在采样周期趋零时收敛到平均切片流；(7) 将该构造推广至线性系统，利用可达性归一化坐标和局部可控性Gramian实现精确有限步实现。

Result: 对于单积分器系统，平均反馈使切片Wasserstein距离至目标非增；对高斯端点分布，反馈为仿射、保持高斯性，并准确驱动均值和协方差到预定终端值。识别出依赖分布的增益可使切片Wasserstein距离线性衰减，同时给出控制能量的显式刻画。随机控制器在采样周期趋零时收敛于平均切片流。对线性系统，可达性归一化坐标适用于一致全驱动系统，局部可控性Gramian实现了一般可控系统的精确有限步实现。

Conclusion: 本文建立了切片最优传输与有限时域分布控制之间的桥梁，提出了一套从投影传输映射到可实现反馈控制律的完整构造方法，涵盖了非线性（单积分器）与线性两类动力学系统。理论保证（单调性、高斯保持性、收敛性）与数值实验共同验证了该框架的有效性，为大规模状态空间的分布控制提供了一条计算可行且理论有保证的路径。

Abstract: Distribution steering seeks feedback laws that drive the state law of a dynamical system between prescribed initial and terminal distributions. Optimal transport provides a natural geometric approach, but its implementation generally requires a transport map or coupling in the full state space. Sliced optimal transport avoids this full-dimensional construction through one-dimensional projections. Yet, the resulting projected maps specify only directional displacements and do not by themselves prescribe a realizable feedback law. To this end, we develop a finite-horizon control framework based on sliced optimal transport. At each sampling instant, a projected optimal transport map defines a directional terminal condition, whose minimum-energy realization yields a randomized single-direction controller. Averaging over projection directions gives a deterministic sliced feedback. For the single-integrator dynamics, the averaged feedback makes the sliced Wasserstein distance to the target non-increasing. For Gaussian endpoint laws, it is affine, preserves Gaussianity, and steers the mean and covariance to their prescribed terminal values. We further identify a law-dependent gain that yields linear decay of the sliced Wasserstein distance together with an explicit characterization of the control energy. We also prove that the randomized controller converges to the averaged sliced flow as the sampling period vanishes. Finally, we extend the construction to linear dynamical systems. Reachability-normalized coordinates allow instantaneous realization of the sliced velocity for uniformly fully actuated systems, while local controllability Gramians provide exact finite-step realization for general controllable systems. Numerical examples illustrate the resulting distributional flows.

</details>


### [107] [Distance-Based Formation Control with Prescribed Performance for Higher-Order Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12896)
*Janina Schaa,Maria Charitidou,Dimos Dimarogonas,Thomas Berger*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对具有未知非线性动力学且相对阶大于1的多智能体系统，提出分布式距离编队控制方法，通过引入虚拟领导者并采用无模型控制律，在树图和最小无限刚性图上实现预设瞬态性能的编队和跟踪控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式距离编队控制大多假设智能体动力学为一阶或二阶线性模型，难以处理未知非线性动力学和相对阶大于1的复杂系统。同时，已有方法缺乏对瞬态性能（如收敛速度和超调）的定量保证，需要发展模型无关的控制方法以处理更一般的多智能体系统。

Method: 1) 引入虚拟领导者，其轨迹由指定智能体子集以预设瞬态行为进行追踪；2) 考虑无向树图或最小且无限刚性图两类通信拓扑；3) 针对后者进一步考虑质心跟踪目标；4) 设计分布式且无模型的控制律，建立编队误差和跟踪误差满足预设漏斗（funnel）约束的保证，并证明所有闭环信号有界；5) 通过数值仿真验证所提控制律的有效性。

Result: 所提出的分布式无模型控制律在两种通信拓扑下均能保证编队和跟踪误差满足预设的漏斗约束，且所有闭环信号有界，数值仿真证实了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为相对阶大于1的未知非线性多智能体系统提供了一种无模型的分布式距离编队控制方案，在保证预设瞬态性能的同时扩展了可处理的系统类别，为后续相关研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We consider distributed distance-based formation control with prescribed transient performance for multi-agent systems modeled by unknown nonlinear dynamics of relative degree greater than one. We introduce a virtual leader whose trajectory is tracked with prescribed transient behavior by a designated subset of agents. The undirected communication graph is either a tree graph or a minimally and infinitesimally rigid graph. In the latter case, we further consider the objective of centroid tracking. In each setting, a distributed and model-free control law is developed, and we establish the satisfaction of the prescribed funnel constraints on the formation and tracking errors, and boundedness of all closed-loop signals. Numerical simulations illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed control laws.

</details>


### [108] [Optimizing Multi-Stakeholder Fairness in Computed Tomography Appointment Scheduling with Predicted Scan and Reporting Duration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13059)
*Ludovico Ambrosi,Chandra Bortolotto,Sara Cambiaghi,Luisa Carone,Davide Duma,Lorenzo Preda*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种预测-决策框架，用于公平感知的随访CT调度。该框架结合患者特异性机器学习模型预测检查和报告时长，并将预测嵌入到多目标混合整数线性规划模型中，同时最小化与患者首选检查日期的偏差并通过字典序最小-最大公平准则平衡放射科医生的报告工作量。基于真实急诊放射科数据的实验表明，仅允许1-2天的调度灵活性即可显著改善工作量公平性，且所提出的支配约简策略能消除大部分ε-约束评估而不影响Pareto前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随访CT调度方法主要优化扫描仪利用率或患者等待时间，忽略了报告活动和跨多利益相关者的公平性平衡。实际调度需要同时兼顾两个竞争目标：让患者尽量接近推荐检查日期，同时确保放射科医生工作量的公平分配。现有方法未能同时处理这两个目标，因此需要一个新的框架。

Method: 分两个阶段：首先，开发患者特异性ML模型（如XGBoost）预测检查和报告时长；其次，将这些预测嵌入到多目标MILP模型中，通过字典序min-max公平准则平衡放射科医生工作负载，同时最小化与患者首选日期的偏差。在ε-约束框架内推导了一个支配约简性质，可大幅减少生成Pareto前沿所需的优化问题数量。

Result: 基于真实急诊放射科数据的计算实验表明：仅允许1-2天的患者调度灵活性即可在保持随访检查及时性的同时显著改善放射科医生的工作量公平性；支配约简策略消除了大部分ε-约束评估且不损害Pareto前沿；通过下游优化遗憾评估预测模型发现XGBoost最有效，优于在传统预测指标上误差更低的模型。

Conclusion: 将预测与优化相结合（预测-决策范式）可为公平感知的随访CT调度提供有效支持；在评估预测模型时，应关注其对下游决策（优化遗憾）的影响，而非仅依赖传统预测误差指标；适度的调度灵活性是改善利益相关者公平性的关键杠杆。

Abstract: Scheduling follow-up Computed Tomography (CT) examinations requires balancing two competing objectives: assigning patients as close as possible to their recommended examination dates while ensuring an equitable distribution of radiologists' workload. Existing approaches optimize scanner utilization or patient waiting times, overlooking reporting activities and the need to balance fairness across multiple stakeholders. This paper proposes a predictive-prescriptive framework for fairness-aware follow-up CT scheduling. Patient-specific Machine Learning (ML) models are first developed to predict both examination and reporting durations. These predictions are then embedded into a multi-objective Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) model that simultaneously minimizes deviations from patients' preferred examination dates and balances radiologists' reporting workloads through a lexicographic min-max fairness criterion. We derive a dominance reduction property within an $ε$-constraint framework that substantially reduces the number of optimization problems required to generate the Pareto frontier. Computational experiments based on data from a real-world emergency radiology department show that allowing patients a scheduling flexibility of only one to two days is sufficient to substantially improve workload equity among radiologists while preserving timely access to follow-up examinations. The proposed dominance reduction strategy eliminates most $ε$-constraint evaluations without affecting the Pareto frontier. Finally, evaluating predictive models through downstream optimization regret demonstrates that XGBoost provides the most effective support for scheduling decisions, outperforming models that achieve lower prediction errors according to conventional predictive metrics.

</details>


### [109] [Positive stabilization of a pure diffusion system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13203)
*Violaine Piengeon,Joseph J. Winkin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对带点观测的一般边界控制扩散系统，对正镇定、正状态估计和基于观测器的正镇定三个问题进行了完整的解析研究，并提出了观测器反馈镇定策略，同时在连续和离散化两种设定下给出了显式可实现的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决扩散系统在正性约束下的镇定和状态估计问题，这些约束在实际工程中广泛存在（如浓度、温度等物理量必须非负），但传统控制理论方法难以系统化地处理这类约束。

Method: 对一般边界控制扩散系统进行解析研究，推导三个正性问题的解析最优解，并针对任意阶空间离散化的PDE系统进行分析以获得数值实现方案，同时与连续系统解进行严格比较。

Result: 三个正性问题（正镇定、正状态估计、基于观测器的正镇定）均获得了解析解，离散化系统的最优解与连续系统解可进行严格比较，且所有解都是显式且可直接实现的。

Conclusion: 该工作为正扩散系统在正性约束下的镇定与估计提供了完整、可实现的理论框架，弥合了连续PDE理论与数值离散实现之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: This paper provides a complete analytical study of the positive stabilization, positive state estimation, and observer-based positive stabilization problems for a generic boundary control diffusion system with point observation. In particular, optimal design problems are solved analytically, and an original strategy for observer-based feedback stabilization is proposed. The three problems are also investigated for an arbitrary-order spatial discretization of the nominal PDE system enabling numerical implementation together with rigorous comparisons with the nominal solutions. In both the continuous and discretized settings, explicit and readily implementable solutions are derived.

</details>


### [110] [A stabilized sequential quadratic programming method for degenerate nonlinear optimization problems on Riemannian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13298)
*Yuya Yamakawa,Mamoru Oka*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对黎曼流形上退化约束优化问题的稳定化序列二次规划（SQP）方法。该方法扩展了欧氏空间中的稳定化SQP框架，在无需任何约束规范（constraint qualification）假设的情况下，证明了生成的序列存在收敛点，该收敛点为KKT点、近似KKT（AKKT）点或相关可行性问题的稳定点。数值实验验证了该方法在退化问题上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的黎曼SQP方法在约束规范成立的条件下才能保证全局收敛，但对于约束规范失效的退化问题（如等式和不等式约束同时存在且经典约束规范不再成立的情况），其收敛行为无法得到保证。因此，需要一种不依赖约束规范假设的、适用于退化问题的SQP方法。

Method: 将欧氏空间中的稳定化SQP框架推广到黎曼流形上，针对带等式和不等式约束的黎曼非线性规划问题（RNLP），在不需要任何约束规范假设的前提下，设计稳定化机制以处理退化约束，并给出全局收敛性分析。

Result: 不假设任何约束规范，证明生成的迭代序列存在聚点，该聚点为KKT点、近似KKT（AKKT）点或相关可行性问题的稳定点；数值实验确认了该方法在退化问题上的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的稳定化SQP方法成功将退化约束优化问题的求解能力从欧氏空间拓展到黎曼流形，在无需约束规范的条件下仍能保证全局收敛性质，为黎曼流形上的退化约束优化提供了一种可靠且有效的求解方案。

Abstract: We propose a stabilized sequential quadratic programming (SQP) method for degenerate constrained optimization problems on Riemannian manifolds. The problem considered in this study is a Riemannian nonlinear programming problem (RNLP) with equality and inequality constraints, where classical constraint qualifications may fail. While existing Riemannian SQP methods guarantee global convergence only under constraint qualifications, their convergence behavior is not ensured for degenerate problems. To address this limitation, we extend the stabilized SQP framework from Euclidean spaces to Riemannian manifolds. Without assuming any constraint qualification, we prove that the generated sequence has an accumulation point that is a Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) point, an approximate KKT (AKKT) point, or a stationary point of an associated feasibility problem. Finally, we conduct numerical experiments to confirm the effectiveness of the proposed method for degenerate problems.

</details>


### [111] [Distributed and Dynamic Hub Network Operation Planning in a Hyperconnected Less-Than-Truckload Operating System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13419)
*Tiankuo Zhang,Jihye Jung,Paria Nourmohammadi,Benoit Montreuil,Alan Erera,Sahrish Jaleel Shaikh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对零担运输（LTL）行业运营效率低、成本高的问题，基于物理互联网（PI）原理，设计了多枢纽运营规划协议，实现多个区域枢纽网络之间的协作，通过智能规划的站内与站间操作提升整个枢纽网络系统的效率与可持续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LTL运输虽比整车运输具有更好的货物整合灵活性，但由于中小型承运商的分散网络和低效运营，平均成本显著更高。现有研究缺乏针对多枢纽网络间协作运营的系统性协议设计，需要一种基于PI原则（模块化集装箱和开放资源共享）的分布式动态枢纽系统来改善这一局面。

Method: 论文基于物理互联网（PI）原则构建分布式动态物流枢纽网络系统，聚焦于枢纽间与枢纽内操作。研究中设计了多枢纽操作规划协议集，供分布式枢纽运营商分别规划：1）枢纽内货物到目的专属拖车的分配；2）枢纽间拖车的发车调度。通过精心设计的枢纽网络间互联机制，实现多枢纽网络的合作效果。

Result: 通过在多情景下对运营商多枢纽网络进行基于仿真的实验，验证了所提出协议的有效性，表明该协议能够有效支持枢纽网络间的合作并完成运输请求。

Conclusion: 本研究提升了基于PI的枢纽网络的实用性和鲁棒性，促进了枢纽网络间的更大合作，有助于构建更高效、更可持续的物流系统。

Abstract: The less-than-truckload (LTL) industry plays a vital role in enhancing the efficiency and sustainability of logistics systems, as LTL shipments offer greater consolidation opportunities than full-truckload shipments. Despite of this flexibility, the average cost of LTL shipments remains considerably higher due to less efficient operations and highly fragmented networks of small and medium-sized carriers. Building on our ongoing effort to develop a distributed and dynamic logistics hub network system grounded in the Physical Internet (PI) principles of modular containers and open resource sharing, this study focuses specifically on inter-hub and in-hub operations, with cooperation among multiple regional hub networks. Therefore, a shipment may traverse multiple cooperating hub networks. With respect to each hub network each shipment enters, it is defined by its expected arrival time at the entry hub and its latest arrival time at the exit hub. Based on the defined shipment information, we design a set of multi-hub operation planning protocols for distributed hub operators. In their operating networks, operators use our smartly designed protocol separately to plan in-hub shipments' assignments to destination-specific trailers and inter-hub trailers' dispatch schedules. With carefully designed interconnections between hub networks, the aggregated hub network system is well-positioned to achieve cooperative outcomes and fulfill shipment requests. We evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed protocol through a simulation-based experiment under multiple scenarios in an operator's multi-hub network. Overall, this research improves the practicality and robustness of PI-based networks and supports greater cooperation among hub networks toward more efficient and sustainable logistics systems.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Monodromy of plane curve singularities and quiver mutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12484)
*Roger Casals*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个平面曲线奇点的可塑性实Morsification的箭图突变类唯一确定其积分单值模。特别地，可塑性divide的箭图突变类唯一确定不可约平面曲线奇点的复拓扑类型，从而在可塑性不可约情形下建立了Fomin-Pylyavskyy-Shustin-Thurston猜想中的代数到拓扑蕴含关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决Fomin、Pylyavskyy、Shustin和Thurston提出的猜想，该猜想声称一个平面曲线奇点的实Morsification（或divide）的组合数据应当完全决定其拓扑类型。作者希望建立从代数/组合数据（箭图突变类）到拓扑数据（单值模）的蕴含关系。

Method: 作者发展了基于表示论的技术，核心工具是微分双分次Ginzburg代数上连续有限维dg模的导出范畴中的等变Euler配对。关键步骤是利用这些技术证明plabic fence的箭图突变类唯一恢复相应光滑链环的Alexander模的挠部分。

Result: 主要结果是：可塑性实Morsification的箭图突变类唯一确定平面曲线奇点的积分单值模；在可塑性不可约情形下，该突变类完全决定奇点的复拓扑类型，证明了几何格拉斯曼簇相关猜想在可塑性不可约情形下的成立。

Conclusion: 该工作通过引入等变Euler配对等表示论工具，成功建立了箭图突变类与奇点拓扑不变量之间的精确对应关系，为Fomin等人猜想的证明提供了重要进展，并为研究实Morsification与链环拓扑之间的联系提供了新的方法论框架。

Abstract: The main result of this article shows that the quiver mutation class of a malleable real Morsification uniquely determines the integral monodromy module of a plane curve singularity. In particular, we show that the quiver mutation class of a malleable divide determines the complex topological type of an irreducible plane curve singularity. This establishes the algebraic-to-topological implication of a conjecture of S.~Fomin, P.~Pylyavskyy, E.~Shustin and D.~Thurston in the malleable irreducible case. The result is proven by developing representation-theoretic techniques based on an equivariant Euler pairing in the derived category of continuous finite-dimensional dg modules over a differential bigraded Ginzburg algebra. A key step uses these techniques to show that the quiver mutation class of a plabic fence uniquely recovers the torsion part of the Alexander module of the associated smooth link.

</details>


### [113] [Virasoro Recursions for Simplicially Stable Curves with Colliding Markings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12507)
*You-Cheng Chou,Hsian-Hua Tseng*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了单纯稳定曲线模叠上的后代积分。对每个有限碰撞复形，将约化态射分解为基本墙穿越，并得到用普通Witten-Kontsevich关联子表示的重构公式，由此推导出修正的Virasoro递归。所有有限支撑理论被封装在完备的不相交支撑Fock空间中，其中扩展势是Witten-Kontsevich势的累积量平移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究单纯稳定曲线模叠上的后代积分，建立其与经典Witten-Kontsevich理论之间的联系，以获得重构公式和Virasoro递归关系，从而深化对这些模空间相交理论的理解。

Method: 将约化态射分解为基本墙穿越（elementary wall crossings），利用这些分解推导后代积分的重构公式；将结果封装在完备的不相交支撑Fock空间中，利用累积量平移构造扩展势。

Result: 得到了以普通Witten-Kontsevich关联子表示的重构公式，推导了修正的Virasoro递归，并将所有有限支撑理论统一封装在一个Fock空间中，扩展势表现为Witten-Kontsevich势的累积量平移。

Conclusion: 单纯稳定曲线模叠的后代积分理论可归结为经典Witten-Kontsevich理论的变形，通过墙穿越分解和累积量平移实现了统一的代数结构刻画。

Abstract: We study descendant integrals on moduli stacks of simplicially stable curves. For each finite collision complex, we factor the reduction morphism into elementary wall crossings and obtain a reconstruction formula in terms of ordinary Witten-Kontsevich correlators. We use the formula to derive corrected Virasoro recursions. We then package all finite-support theories in a completed disjoint-support Fock space, where the extended potential is a cumulant translate of the Witten-Kontsevich potential.

</details>


### [114] [Collision-Hull Compression for Homogeneous Keller Maps and a Forty-Variable Counterexample to Zhao's Vanishing Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12543)
*Thomas Prellberg*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于碰撞的初等压缩原理，适用于齐次Keller映射。通过极化代数语言，构造由碰撞生成的子代数，该子代数携带非单射的Keller限制并控制标准对称提升的维数。对于Thompson的24变量三次齐次映射，精确极化给出生长序列2,4,11,20,20，所得子代数恰为MacFarlane的20维不变子空间，从而从碰撞本身规范地恢复了已知的24到20压缩。文章还给出了40变量提升的显式350项齐次四次式，使Zhao消失猜想失效，并给出在Q(i)上的精确梯度碰撞。在Q、Q(i)和C上，在未改变的对称提升之前不存在包含该碰撞的真不变线性子空间。作者未声称全局最小性，且主要显式计算由伴随的精确计算验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Keller映射是Jacobian猜想的核心研究对象。先前已知Thompson的24变量三次齐次映射可压缩为20维不变子空间，但该压缩的机制尚不清晰。本文旨在揭示这一压缩的规范来源——即碰撞本身（collision），从而为理解Keller映射的结构压缩提供更基本原理，并检验Zhao消失猜想的有效性。

Method: 作者建立了一个初等的碰撞生成压缩原理：针对齐次Keller映射，按下列方式进行构造——(1) 在极化代数语言中，取由碰撞生成的子代数；(2) 证明该子代数携带一个非单射的Keller限制；(3) 利用该限制控制标准对称提升的维数。通过对Thompson映射进行精确极化计算，获得生长序列并识别出所得子代数。随后构建显式的350单项式齐次四次式以检验Zhao消失猜想，并在Q(i)上给出精确梯度碰撞。

Result: (1) Thompson的24变量三次齐次映射经精确极化得到生长2,4,11,20,20，所得子代数恰为MacFarlane的20维不变子空间，24到20的压缩被规范地从碰撞本身恢复。(2) 构造了40变量提升下的350单项式齐次四次式，使Zhao消失猜想失效。(3) 在Q(i)上存在精确梯度碰撞。(4) 在Q、Q(i)和C上，在未改变的对称提升之前不存在包含该碰撞的真不变线性子空间。

Conclusion: 碰撞本身足以规范性解释Thompson映射的24到20维压缩，提供了压缩的新原理。Zhao消失猜想不成立。作者谨慎地未声明全局最小性，且所有显式计算均以精确伴随计算验证。

Abstract: We formulate an elementary collision-generated compression principle for homogeneous Keller maps. In the language of polarization algebras, the construction takes the subalgebra generated by a collision; the additional point is that this subalgebra carries a noninjective Keller restriction and controls the dimension of the standard symmetric lift. For Thompson's 24-variable cubic-homogeneous map, exact polarization gives growth \(2,4,11,20,20\), and the resulting subalgebra is exactly MacFarlane's 20-dimensional invariant subspace. Thus the known \(24\)-to-\(20\) compression is recovered canonically from the collision itself. Although the resulting 40-variable lift had already been noted, we give an explicit 350-monomial homogeneous quartic for which Zhao's Vanishing Conjecture fails, together with an exact gradient collision over \(\Q(i)\). Over \(\Q\), \(\Q(i)\), and \(\C\), no proper invariant linear subspace containing this collision is available before the unchanged symmetric lift. No global minimality is claimed. The principal explicit calculations are checked by an accompanying exact calculation.

</details>


### [115] [Rigidity for Lie algebras of locally finite derivations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12567)
*Mohamed Ali Belabbas*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了：特征零域上有限生成交换代数的任何由局部有限推导生成的有限生成Lie子代数必是有限维的。作为推论，刻画了有限个局部有限推导生成的Lie子代数的等价条件；并独立地证明了由局部幂零推导组成的Lie子代数必可解（约化情形下导长以dim A为界且最优）。由此回答了Popov在2005年提出的问题，并给出多项式控制系统可精确有限维双线性实现的特征刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机在于联系推导的局部有限/局部幂零性质与Lie代数结构的有限维性，解决如Popov提出的关于自同构群中由单参数子群生成子群的代数性问题，以及多项式控制系统的有限维双线性实现问题。

Method: 作者引入一个共限理想，使其与Spec A的每个结合点的闭包相交；在沿相应有限子概形的二阶消失推导所构成的子代数上，局部有限性被证明可强制导出局部幂零性。该子代数与L的交因此由第二个定理可知是可解的，且在L中具有有限余维；结合伴随作用的局部有限性，最终得出L的有限维性。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 有限生成Lie子代数L⊆Der_K(A)若其元素在A上均局部有限，则L有限维；(2) 由有限个局部有限推导生成的Lie子代数，有限维、在A上局部有限作用、所有元素局部有限三者等价；(3) 由局部幂零推导构成的Lie子代数必可解，约化情形下导长≤dim A且界最优；(4) 回答了Popov 2005年关于两个幺单单参数子群的问题；(5) 给出多项式控制系统精确有限维双线性实现的刻画。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入共限理想与二阶消失子代数这一关键技术，建立了局部有限性与局部幂零性之间的联系，从而统一解决了代数几何（自同构群）、Lie代数理论和控制理论中的多个问题，表明了局部有限推导代数结构的刚性。

Abstract: Let $A$ be a finitely generated commutative algebra over a field $\mathbb K$ of characteristic zero. We prove that every finitely generated Lie subalgebra $L\subseteq\operatorname{Der}_{\mathbb K}(A)$ whose elements are locally finite on $A$ is finite-dimensional. Consequently, for a Lie subalgebra generated by finitely many locally finite derivations, the following are equivalent: it is finite-dimensional, it acts locally finitely on $A$, and all its elements are locally finite. A key ingredient is a second theorem of independent interest: every Lie subalgebra of $\operatorname{Der}_{\mathbb K}(A)$ whose elements are locally nilpotent is solvable; when $A$ is reduced, its derived length is at most $\dim A$, and this bound is sharp. For an affine variety $X$ over an algebraically closed field, we deduce that a subgroup of $\operatorname{Aut}(X)$ generated by finitely many connected algebraic subgroups is algebraic if and only if every element of the Lie algebra generated by their tangent algebras is locally finite. For two unipotent one-parameter subgroups, this provides an answer to a problem posed by Popov in 2005. Our results also characterize polynomial control systems admitting an exact finite-dimensional bilinear realization by polynomial observables containing the state coordinates. The proofs rest on the introduction of a cofinite ideal meeting the closure of every associated point of $\operatorname{Spec} A$: on the subalgebra of derivations vanishing to second order along the corresponding finite subscheme, local finiteness forces local nilpotence. The intersection of that subalgebra with $L$ is therefore solvable by the second theorem, and has finite codimension in $L$; together with local finiteness of the adjoint action, this yields finite-dimensionality.

</details>


### [116] [Tame fundamental groups of rigid spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12601)
*Piotr Achinger,Katharina Hübner,Marcin Lara,Jakob Stix*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文引入了刚性空间在非阿基米德域K上的tame étale基本群π_1^t(X/K)，证明了在qcqs条件下且K具有拓扑有限生成的tame Galois群（如代数闭域或局部域）时，该基本群拓扑有限生成；若X进一步是严格半稳定形式概形的刚性一般纤维且其特殊纤维的光滑闭包允许射影snc紧化，则该基本群拓扑有限展示。证明依赖于对数几何技术（推广到非有限生成幺半群）、tame log étale基本群的类比有限性结果，以及adic空间映射的'竖直紧化'。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非阿基米德几何中，基础群的结构与有限性性质对理解刚性空间的拓扑和几何性质至关重要。此前关于刚性空间基本群的有限性结果有限，特别是tame étale基本群的概念尚不明确。本文旨在建立tame étale基本群，并证明其在自然条件下具有拓扑有限生成甚至有限展示的性质，从而为刚性空间的代数几何与算术几何研究提供新的工具。

Method: 本文采用三种关键技术手段：(1) 将对数几何技术从通常的有限生成幺半群推广到更广泛的范畴；(2) 建立tame log étale基本群的类比有限性结果，并利用其与tame étale基本群的联系；(3) 使用adic空间的'竖直紧化'（vertical compactification）方法，将关于形式概形特殊纤维的信息转化为关于刚性一般纤维的基本群信息。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 若X是qcqs刚性空间且K的tame Galois群拓扑有限生成，则π_1^t(X/K)拓扑有限生成；(2) 在更严格的条件下（严格半稳定形式概形的刚性一般纤维，且特殊纤维光滑位形具有射影snc紧化），π_1^t(X/K)拓扑有限展示。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了刚性空间tame étale基本群的有限性理论，并将对数几何方法推广至更一般的幺半群设定。这为理解非阿基米德刚性空间的算术基本群结构提供了重要基础，其结果覆盖了代数闭域和局部域等典型算术场景。

Abstract: We introduce the tame étale fundamental group $π_1^t(X/K)$ of a rigid space X over a non-archimedean field K. We show that if X is qcqs and K has topologically finitely generated tame Galois group (e.g. algebraically closed or a local field), then $π_1^t(X/K)$ is topologically finitely generated. If X is moreover the rigid generic fibre of a strictly semistable formal scheme such that the smooth locus of its special fibre admits a projective snc compactification, then $π_1^t(X/K)$ is topologically finitely presented. The proofs rely on techniques of logarithmic geometry (extended beyond its usual scope of finitely generated monoids), in particular on an analogous finiteness statement for the tame log étale fundamental group, and on the 'vertical compactification' of a map of adic spaces.

</details>


### [117] [Lower bounds for the Smith-Thom deficiency of Hilbert squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12644)
*Viatcheslav Kharlamov,Rareş Răsdeaconu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文建立了极大非奇异实射影簇的Hilbert平方的Smith–Thom亏格下界，这些下界恢复了已知的曲面情形，并在所有维数≥2的情况下提供了Smith–Thom极大性的新障碍。作为应用，证明了任意维数≥2的实阿贝尔簇的Hilbert平方不是Smith–Thom极大的，并进一步证明了极大实阿贝尔簇的Hilbert平方的亏格随维数指数增长，同时还获得了某些笛卡尔积的Hilbert平方的非极大性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究实代数簇的Hilbert平方的Smith–Thom亏格下界，旨在通过建立新的数值障碍来判定实射影簇的Smith–Thom极大性，特别是填补除曲面之外更高维情形的空白。

Method: 通过数学证明的方法，针对极大非奇异实射影簇的Hilbert平方建立Smith–Thom亏格的下界估计，并结合具体应用（如实阿贝尔簇和笛卡尔积）推导出非极大性结论。

Result: 恢复了已知的曲面情形结果；在所有维数≥2的情形下提供了新的Smith–Thom极大性障碍；证明了维数≥2的实阿贝尔簇的Hilbert平方不是Smith–Thom极大的；极大实阿贝尔簇的Hilbert平方的亏格随维数指数增长；获得了某些笛卡尔积的Hilbert平方的非极大性结果。

Conclusion: Hilbert平方的Smith–Thom亏格为实代数簇的极大性提供了有效且随维数增长迅速的新不变量，尤其对实阿贝尔簇族给出了指数级的非极大性证据。

Abstract: We establish lower bounds for the Smith--Thom deficiency of the Hilbert square of a maximal nonsingular real projective variety. These bounds recover the known surface case and provide new obstructions to Smith-Thom maximality in every dimension at least two. As applications, we prove that the Hilbert square of any real abelian variety of dimension at least two is not Smith-Thom maximal. We further show that the deficiencies of the Hilbert squares of maximal real abelian varieties grow exponentially with the dimension. We also obtain nonmaximality results for the Hilbert squares of certain Cartesian products.

</details>


### [118] [Topological obstructions to geometric positivity and negativity on Calabi-Yau manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12705)
*Ping Li*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Calabi-Yau流形的拓扑结构能否支持自然的几何正性或负性结构。在偶复维数n≥4（当n≥6时假设b2=1）下，强化了Oguiso-Peternell定理，证明Calabi-Yau流形不可能是弱Fano n-折叠的同胚流形；该阻碍同样适用于具有拟正全纯截面曲率的Kähler流形。变换群类比排除了具有哈密顿圆作用且具有孤立不动点的辛流形。在负性方面，证明了Calabi-Yau流形的基本群不与Kähler双曲流形的基本群同构。这些结果共同展现了将Calabi-Yau流形与由几何正性或负性支配的若干基本类别区分开的拓扑刚性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望深入理解Calabi-Yau流形的拓扑刚性，探究其底层拓扑是否可能承载自然的几何正性（如Fano、全纯截面曲率正）或负性（如Kähler双曲）结构，从而进一步刻画Calabi-Yau流形在代数几何与微分几何中的独特地位。

Method: 作者采用代数拓扑与微分几何相结合的方法。在正性方面，通过强化Oguiso-Peternell的经典结果，利用全纯截面曲率的几何约束与拓扑不变量之间的相互作用；在负性方面，利用基本群的代数性质来排除Calabi-Yau流形与Kähler双曲流形的同构可能性。此外，还通过变换群方法处理具有哈密顿圆作用的辛流形情形。

Result: 在偶复维数n≥4（n≥6时假设b2=1）下，证明了Calabi-Yau流形不与弱Fano n-折叠同胚；该阻碍同样适用于具有拟正全纯截面曲率的Kähler流形。变换群类比排除了具有孤立不动点的哈密顿圆作用辛流形。还证明了Calabi-Yau流形的基本群不与Kähler双曲流形的基本群同构。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：Calabi-Yau流形在拓扑层面既不支持自然的几何正性结构，也不支持几何负性结构，从而展现出一种普遍的拓扑刚性，将其与由正性或负性几何支配的多种基本流形类别明确区分开来。

Abstract: We study whether the topology underlying a Calabi-Yau manifold can support natural geometric positivity or negativity structures. In even complex dimension $n \geq 4$ (assuming $b_2=1$ when $n \geq 6$), we strengthen a theorem of Oguiso-Peternell by proving that a Calabi-Yau manifold is not homeomorphic to a weak Fano $n$-fold. The same obstruction applies to Kähler manifolds with quasi-positive holomorphic sectional curvature. A transformation-group analogue excludes, in particular, symplectic manifolds admitting Hamiltonian circle actions with isolated fixed points. On the negative side, we show that the fundamental group of a Calabi-Yau manifold is not isomorphic to that of a Kähler hyperbolic manifold. Taken together, these results exhibit a common topological rigidity separating Calabi-Yau manifolds from several fundamental classes governed by geometric positivity or negativity.

</details>


### [119] [Supersymmetry, differential operators of infinite order and theta functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12846)
*Mikhail Kapranov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文利用超对称性重新诠释了Sato于1972年提出的通过无穷阶微分算子（DOI）证明Theta零值形式模性的方法，发现任何奇超对称生成元的自然指数即是一种DOI。具体而言，亏格n的Riemann theta函数由超群OSp(1|2n)（及其metaplectic覆盖）在Siegel平面的超加厚化上作用所支配；n=2时对应三维N=1超共形群，等价于自由无质量标量超多重态；n>2时得到广义共形结构的超扩展。此外，核上（on-shell）的奇超对称生成元满足偶型Heisenberg交换关系，而运动方程升级为对应于osp(1|2n)超Weil表示的BGG型分解的微分算子复形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sato在1972年提出了一种通过仅依赖模变量本身的无穷阶微分算子（DOI）来证明诸如Theta零值形式模性的方法，该方法后来被Kashiwara、Kawai、Takei和Yoshida等人发展。作者希望给出这一方法的自然解释，并找到DOIs的天然来源。

Method: 作者将超对称性引入Sato的方法：任何奇超对称生成元的自然指数（naive exponential）都会产生DOI。对于亏格n的Riemann theta函数，作者考虑超群OSp(1|2n)（及其metaplectic覆盖）在Siegel平面超加厚化上的作用。对n=2，这与3维N=1超共形群对应，等价于自由无质量标量超多重态（同时包含Laplace和Dirac方程）；对n>2，则得到Lagrangian Grassmannian（如同任何Hermitian对称空间）上广义共形结构的超扩展。作者还研究了核上奇超对称生成元满足偶型Heisenberg交换关系的特性。

Result: 给出了Sato的DOI方法在超对称框架下的自然解释：奇超对称生成元的指数即为DOI。建立了Riemann theta函数与OSp(1|2n)超群作用之间的联系，n=2时精确对应自由无质量标量超多重态（Laplace与Dirac方程的结合），n>2时推广到广义共形结构的超扩展。同时发现核上奇超对称生成元满足偶型Heisenberg对易关系，运动方程可升级为微分算子复形。

Conclusion: 超对称性为Sato的DOI方法提供了自然的物理来源和结构解释。该框架不仅统一了不同亏格情形（n=2的N=1超共形情形与n>2的广义共形超扩展），而且通过BGG型分解揭示了osp(1|2n)超Weil表示背后的微分算子复形结构，为理解Theta零值形式的模性提供了新的数学物理视角。

Abstract: In 1972, M. Sato proposed an approach to proving modularity of forms like Thetanullwerte by characterizing them via certain differential operators of infinite order (DOI) in the modular variable(s) alone. A DOI is an infinite series in derivatives decreasing so fast that it acts on holomorphic functions by a sheaf morphism. This approach was developed by several authors including Kashiwara, Kawai, Takei and Yoshida.
  We give an interpretation of this approach using supersymmetry which provides a natural source of DOIs: the naive exponential of any odd supersymmetry generator is a DOI. The case of the Riemann theta function of genus n is governed by the supergroup OSp(1|2n) (and its metaplectic cover) acting on a natural super-thickening of the Siegel plane. For n=2 this is the 3-dimensional N=1 superconformal group and the structure at hand is precisely the free massless scalar supermultiplet (combining the Laplace and Dirac equations). For n>2 we get a super-extension of the generalized conformal structure existing on the Lagrangian Grassmannian as on any Hermitian symmetric space.
  An additional interesting feature here is that the odd supersymmetry generators acting ``on-shell'' (i.e., in the space of solutions of the equations of motion) satisfy even-style Heisenberg commutation relations. These equations of motion upgrade to a complex of differential operators corresponding to a natural BGG-type resolution of the super-Weil representation of osp(1|2n).

</details>


### [120] [Algebraic cycles and Fano threefolds of genus 7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12950)
*Robert Laterveer*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文研究亏格7的非常一般素数Fano三维簇Y，构造了Y×Y上一个Abel-Jacobi平凡但非挠的2-轮换，从而证明Y不具有Shen-Vial意义下的乘法Chow-Künneth分解。同时证明任何Fano三维簇在代数等价意义下都具有乘法Chow-Künneth分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shen-Vial引入的乘法Chow-Künneth分解是研究代数簇Chow群结构与动机分解的重要工具。判断哪些Fano三维簇满足或违反该性质，对于理解Chow群与Abel-Jacobi映射之间的关系具有重要意义。此前尚无亏格7素数Fano三维簇的类似结果，本文填补了这一空白。

Method: 本文通过非常一般亏格7素数Fano三维簇Y的几何性质（如其中1-cycles与曲面结构），在积簇Y×Y上显式构造一个2-维代数轮换。该轮换被证明在Abel-Jacobi映射下像为0（即Abel-Jacobi平凡），但在Chow群A^4(Y×Y)中不是挠元。利用这一轮换的存在性，结合Shen-Vial的理论框架，反证Y不具备乘法Chow-Künneth分解。此外，针对一般Fano三维簇，作者通过代数等价意义下的构造证明其具备乘法Chow-Künneth分解。

Result: 1) 在亏格7非常一般素数Fano三维簇Y的Y×Y上，存在一个显式构造的2-轮换，它是Abel-Jacobi平凡的但在Chow群A^4(Y×Y)中非挠。2) 由此推出Y不承认乘法Chow-Künneth分解。3) 反之，任何Fano三维簇在代数等价模下均具备乘法Chow-Künneth分解（即模代数等价的Chow-Künneth分解具有乘法性）。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了亏格7素数Fano三维簇在Chow理论层面的特殊行为：虽然其模代数等价的Chow群具有乘法分解结构，但在整Chow群层面乘法Chow-Künneth分解不成立。这为Shen-Vial乘法分解理论提供了新的反例，并加深了对Fano三维簇动机分解与Chow群结构之间关系的理解。

Abstract: Let $Y$ be a very general prime Fano threefold of genus 7. We exhibit an explicit 2-cycle on $Y\times Y$ that is Abel-Jacobi trivial but non-torsion in the Chow group $A^4(Y\times Y)$. As a consequence, $Y$ does not admit a multiplicative Chow-Künneth decomposition, in the sense of Shen-Vial. We also show that any Fano threefold has a multiplicative Chow-Künneth decomposition modulo algebraic equivalence.

</details>


### [121] [Tropical and algebraic elliptic plane curves with fixed j-invariant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13081)
*Alessio Cela,Sae Koyama*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文对具有固定j不变量的平面良间距椭圆曲线进行热带计数，并结合Cela-Koyama的亏格1对应定理，给出了Pandharipande代数枚举的另一种证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在通过热带几何方法重新推导Pandharipande关于亏格1椭圆曲线的代数计数结果，从而提供一种独立于原有代数几何方法的证明路径，验证热带-代数对应定理的效力。

Method: 利用热带几何中的计数技术，对平面良间距（well-spaced）椭圆曲线在固定j不变量条件下进行枚举，再结合Cela-Koyama近期建立的亏格1对应定理，将热带计数结果对应到代数几何中的计数。

Result: 成功通过热带计数方法重新得到Pandharipande的代数枚举公式，为该经典结果提供了新的证明。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了热带几何在亏格1曲线计数问题上的有效性，并通过对应定理搭建了热带计数与代数计数之间的桥梁，为相关枚举问题提供了新方法。

Abstract: We tropically enumerate planar well-spaced elliptic curves with a fixed $j$-invariant. Combined with the recent genus 1 correspondence theorem of Cela--Koyama, this yields an alternative proof of Pandharipande's algebraic enumeration.

</details>


### [122] [Equivalences via twisted hyperholomorphic sheaves from transverse Lagrangian fibrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13403)
*Moritz Hartlieb,Saket Shah*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文沿用Kapustka-Kapustka的思路，利用Lagrangian纤维化在K3^[n]型和OG10型超凯勒流形的乘积上构造扭曲超全纯层，并由此证明了OG10型超凯勒流形的Lefschetz标准猜想和D-等价猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超凯勒流形上的标准猜想和导出等价猜想是代数几何与Hodge理论中的核心难题。已有工作（如Kapustka-Kapustka）通过Lagrangian纤维化构造超全纯层来解决K3^[n]型情形，作者希望将该方法推广到更复杂的OG10型超凯勒流形，以证明这些长期未解的猜想。

Method: 作者采用Kapustka-Kapustka提出的技术路线：利用超凯勒流形上的Lagrangian纤维化结构，在K3^[n]型和OG10型超凯勒流形的乘积空间上构造扭曲的（twisted）超全纯层。这些扭曲超全纯层作为桥梁，将产物流形的上同调与Hodge理论联系起来，从而为证明Lefschetz标准猜想和D-等价猜想提供关键工具。

Result: 成功构造了所需的扭曲超全纯层，并以此为基础证明了OG10型超凯勒流形上的Lefschetz标准猜想以及D-等价猜想（即导出等价性与某些Hodge理论性质等价的猜想）。

Conclusion: 该工作将Kapustka-Kapustka的方法成功推广至OG10型超凯勒流形，为解决超凯勒流形上的标准猜想体系提供了新的有效工具，并进一步验证了Lagrangian纤维化在导出几何与Hodge理论中的深刻作用。

Abstract: Following ideas of Kapustka-Kapustka, we use Lagrangian fibrations to construct twisted hyperholomorphic sheaves on products of hyperkähler manifolds of K3$^{[n]}$- and OG10-type. As applications, we prove the Lefschetz standard conjecture and the D-equivalence conjecture for hyperkähler manifolds of OG10-type.

</details>


### [123] [Sites and Grothendieck Topologies, Sites and Sheaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13405)
*Arvid Siqveland*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文提供了理解Grothendieck topos定义与应用所需的极简范畴论基础，阐述了site层的基本性质，并将其应用于模空间理论，最终证明对具有特定性质的范畴C可构造一个proper categories的scheme范畴（而非如stack那样的2-范畴）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grothendieck topos是代数几何中Stacks与模空间理论的核心工具，但完整范畴论门槛较高。作者旨在以最小的范畴论预备知识介绍topos，并解决模空间研究中"对象之方案"的范畴构造问题——传统上使用stacks会得到2-范畴，作者希望通过特定条件避免这一复杂性。

Method: 首先介绍site（格罗滕迪克拓扑）与sheaf的定义及基本性质，建立必要的最小范畴论框架；随后将这些工具应用于模空间理论；最后证明对满足特定显式性质的范畴C，可以构造对象方案范畴（category of schemes of objects），该范畴是proper category而非2-范畴。

Result: 作者成功构建了满足特定性质的范畴C上的对象方案范畴，并严格证明了该范畴是proper category（而非stacks情形下的2-范畴），同时展示了site与sheaf理论在模空间中的应用。

Conclusion: 在适当的条件下，模空间理论中的对象方案可以避免使用2-范畴框架，退化为更简单的proper category构造，从而简化了相关范畴论结构。

Abstract: We give the minimum of category theory necessary for understanding the definition and applications of Grothendieck topos. We state the basic properties of sites and sheaves, and give applications to the theory of moduli. We prove that for categories $\mathbf C$ with explicit stated properties, we can construct a category of schemes of objects in $\mathbf C$ which is a proper category rather than a $2$-category as in the case of stacks.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Black-Box Knowledge Transfer across Distinct Feature Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12403)
*Oh-Ran Kwon,Daeyoung Ham*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从预训练黑箱预测函数向新的异构输入空间迁移预测知识的方法。该方法将目标回归函数分解为可迁移组件（由黑箱提供信息）和不可迁移组件（捕捉新空间独有信息），并通过两步神经网络过程估计这两部分，同时给出了预测风险界与理论保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练黑箱模型蕴含了海量数据与大量计算提炼的知识，但当可用输入特征与黑箱期望的特征不一致时，无法直接使用。因此需要一个方法，能够将黑箱中的预测知识迁移到一个异构的新输入空间中，以充分利用已有模型的知识。

Method: 将目标回归函数分解为可迁移组件和不可迁移组件两部分。提出两步神经网络流程：第一步利用大量无标注的特征对（连接两个输入空间的桥梁）估计可迁移组件；第二步利用有限的标注数据估计不可迁移组件。推导预测风险界，并将框架扩展到多个黑箱（各自主导一个输入空间），通过聚合降低预测误差。

Result: 推导的风险界表明：当不可迁移组件较小或光滑时，方法优于非迁移的替代方案，且能自适应两种情况。在额外条件下，所提估计器的最坏情况风险的阶严格小于仅用标注数据估计的极小极大风险。多黑箱聚合可降低相对最优单黑箱的预测误差，模拟和真实数据验证了方法的实用价值。

Conclusion: 该方法有效实现了从黑箱预测函数到异构输入空间的知识迁移，在理论上具有严格优于非迁移方法的风险保障，并能稳健适应不同复杂度水平的不可迁移组件；多黑箱情景下的聚合进一步提升了预测性能。

Abstract: Pre-trained black-box predictive functions encode knowledge distilled from massive datasets and extensive computation. However, when the available input features differ from those the black box expects, direct use is infeasible. We introduce a method for transferring predictive knowledge from the black box to a new, heterogeneous input space. Our approach decomposes the target regression function into a transferable component, which the black box can inform, and a non-transferable component, which captures information unique to the new space. We propose a two-step neural network procedure, estimating the transferable component from abundant unlabeled feature pairs that bridge the two input spaces and the non-transferable component from limited labels. We derive prediction risk bounds that improve on those of a non-transfer alternative when the non-transferable component is small or smooth, and the procedure adapts to either case. Under additional conditions, the worst-case risk of our estimator is of strictly smaller polynomial order than the minimax risk of estimation from the labeled data alone. We extend the framework to multiple black boxes, each on its own input space, and show that aggregation can reduce prediction error relative to the best single black box. Simulated and real data demonstrate the practical value of the method.

</details>


### [125] [SSPO: Structure-Aware Similarity-Weighted Preference Optimization for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12443)
*Yuanyu Li,Jintao Xu,Zijiang Liu,Yongzhi Qi,Ningxuan Kang,Jianshen Zhang,Wei Qi,Chen Xie,Zuo-Jun Max Shen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: SSPO（结构感知相似性加权偏好优化）通过引入基于不相似性加权的留一法基线，统一评分所有共采样的B个解决方案，同时解决了梯度信号极化和基线冗余两个问题，在TSP、EFL、JSP基准上取得一致改进，并已在京东生产设施选址系统中部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经组合优化（NCO）训练方法未能充分利用共采样解组中丰富的潜在信息：基于偏好优化的方法锚定单个最优解，忽略了其他解的细粒度质量与结构信号，造成梯度信号极化；均值基线则对解统一加权，使得结构近似的解用冗余信息淹没基线并保持高梯度方差，造成基线冗余。

Method: 提出SSPO（结构感知相似性加权偏好优化），对所有B个采样解通过不相似性加权的留一法基线进行联合评分：结构上差异更大的解获得更高权重，用一个统一机制同时解决上述两个缺陷。该基线采用零参数、问题自适应的解嵌入，由编码器现有节点表示构建。

Result: 在TSP、EFL和JSP基准上的实验相比此前的锚定最优解与统一权重基线均取得一致改进。与TSP和EFL上的统一权重RLOO直接对比证实，结构感知加权是改进的主要驱动力。此外，SSPO训练的EFL策略已在京东生产设施选址系统中部署，验证了大规模实际可行性。

Conclusion: 结构感知的相似性加权能够有效克服NCO训练中梯度信号极化与基线冗余两大问题，通过单一机制统一处理所有采样解的细粒度信息，带来一致的性能提升，并具备实际生产部署的价值。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) relies on parallel solution sampling for training, yet existing methods fail to fully exploit the rich information latent in a co-sampled solution group. Preference-optimization methods anchor on the single best solution and discard fine-grained quality and structural signal from all other peers-a failure we term gradient signal polarization. Mean-based baselines instead weight peers uniformly, so structurally near-identical peers flood the baseline with redundant information and keep gradient variance high-a failure we term baseline redundancy. We propose SSPO (Structure-Aware Similarity-Weighted Preference Optimization), which scores all $B$ sampled solutions jointly through a dissimilarity-weighted leave-one-out baseline: structurally distinct peers receive higher weight, resolving both failures in a single mechanism. The baseline uses zero-parameter, problem-adaptive solution embeddings built from the encoder's existing node representations. Experiments on TSP, EFL, and JSP benchmarks show consistent gains over prior best-anchor and uniform-weight baselines. A direct comparison against uniform RLOO on TSP and EFL confirms that structure-aware weighting is the primary driver of improvement. The SSPO-trained EFL policy has been deployed in a production facility-location system at JD$\mathord{.}$com, confirming practical viability at scale.

</details>


### [126] [Online Inference for Quantile Temporal Difference Learning in Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12973)
*Zijie Cheng,Yang Peng,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布强化学习中分位数时序差分学习（QTD）的统计推断问题，在生成模型假设下建立了同步和异步QTD的函数中心极限定理，并提出了基于随机缩放的在线推断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布强化学习中的QTD算法缺乏严格的统计推断理论支持，现有方法通常需要存储整个迭代轨迹，内存开销大且难以支持高效的在线推断。

Method: 首先在生成模型访问假设下，为同步和异步QTD建立函数中心极限定理，证明其平均迭代弱收敛到重标度的布朗运动；随后基于随机缩放方法构造渐近枢轴统计量，利用整个QTD路径的信息进行推断，且该统计量可在线计算，无需存储全部迭代轨迹。

Result: 建立了同步与异步QTD的函数中心极限定理，并提出了可在线计算、不需要存储完整QTD轨迹的渐近枢轴统计量，显著降低了内存需求。

Conclusion: 该工作为分布强化学习提供了有效的统计推断框架，在保证推断精度的同时大幅降低了存储开销，推动了QTD算法在实践中的应用。

Abstract: In this paper, we study how to perform statistical inference for quantile temporal difference learning (QTD) in distributional reinforcement learning. Assuming access to a generative model, we first establish functional central limit theorems for both synchronous and asynchronous QTD, which show that the averaged iterates of QTD converge weakly to a rescaled Brownian motion. We next provide online inference methods. Based on random scaling, the inference procedure constructs an asymptotically pivotal statistic for inference by using the information along the whole QTD path. Meanwhile, the proposed statistic can be computed online without storing the entire trajectory of QTD iterates. This substantially reduces the memory requirement and enables efficient statistical inference in distributional reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [127] [Statistical Properties of Robust Learning under Distributional Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13133)
*Zhiyi Li,Xiaojie Mao,Yunbei Xu,Ruohan Zhan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布偏移（distributional shift）下两种稳健学习框架——分布稳健优化（DRO）和稳健满足（RS）——在目标环境中的有限样本泛化误差，提出了显式的泛化误差界、信息导向的超参数校准方法，并将其应用于网络批量问题（network lot-sizing）以解释稳健策略对需求正偏移的响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRO和RS的有限样本保证要么在源环境中建立，要么针对模糊集上的最坏情况性能进行分析，缺乏对目标（偏移后）环境中泛化误差的系统性刻画与两种方法的比较，这是一个未被充分探索的理论空白。

Method: （1）为DRO和RS推导目标偏移环境下的有限样本泛化误差上界，明确刻画稳健性超参数带来的偏移敏感度降低与正则化惩罚之间的权衡，并避免Wasserstein经验集中的维度灾难；（2）在已知部分偏移信息（如偏移幅度或方向）时，提出信息导向的超参数校准方法，在同一信息条件下系统比较两种方法；（3）将框架应用于网络批量问题，解释稳健策略对需求正偏移的响应。

Result: 推导出了DRO和RS在偏移目标环境下的有限样本泛化误差界；在部分信息场景下，两种方法在理论分析和实证表现上呈现互补性；通过网络批量问题展示了稳健策略对需求正偏移的响应机制。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了分布偏移下稳健学习方法统计性质理解上的空白，为比较DRO和RS提供了原理性基础。

Abstract: Distributional shifts arise when the target deployment environment differs from the source environment that generated the training data. Robust learning frameworks such as Distributionally Robust Optimization (DRO) and Robust Satisficing (RS) aim to address this challenge, yet their finite-sample guarantees under such shifts, and their systematic comparison, remain underexplored: existing analyses typically establish guarantees either in the source environment or for adversarial worst-case performance over an ambiguity set. This paper instead studies generalization error in the target environment---the excess loss under the shifted target distribution. Our contributions are threefold. First, we derive finite-sample generalization error bounds in the shifted target environment for both DRO and RS. These bounds explicitly characterize the trade-off between reduced sensitivity to shift and the regularization penalty induced by each method's robustness hyperparameter, and they avoid the curse of dimensionality associated with Wasserstein empirical concentration. Second, when partial shift information such as shift magnitude or direction is available, we propose information-directed hyperparameter calibrations and compare the two methods given the same information. Under these calibrations, and in the partial-information regimes we study, DRO and RS exhibit complementary theoretical and empirical behavior. Finally, we apply the framework to a network lot-sizing problem, using it to interpret how robust policies respond to positive shifts in the demand distribution. Together, these results fill a gap in understanding the statistical properties of robust learning methods under distributional shifts and provide a principled basis for comparing DRO and RS.

</details>


### [128] [High-dimensional networks and mean squared error for possibly misspecified models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13171)
*Lourens Waldorp*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究高维网络分析中节点邻域选择问题，证明当参数数量远大于观测数量时，最小描述长度（MDL）方法能实现保守的邻域估计（低假阳性率），而传统方法如Lasso、AIC、BIC会导致虚假边。同时将结果与机器学习的"双重下降"现象联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在网络分析中为避免遗漏重要变量，越来越多的变量被纳入分析，导致高维情形（参数远多于观测）下如何正确选择每个节点的邻域（即哪些连接存在于网络中）成为关键问题。传统邻域选择方法在高维下容易产生虚假边（高假阳性率），需要寻找更可靠的方法。

Method: 论文通过理论分析和推导，考察了线性模型和非线性模型两种情形下的邻域选择问题。在线性模型中邻域选择效果良好，在非线性模型中需要针对高维施加惩罚。作者分析了岭参数对均方误差的影响，并引入了最小描述长度（MDL）方法，证明其在高维设置下能实现正确的邻域选择或更小的邻域（低假阳性率）。

Result: 证明了在高维设置下，传统邻域选择方法（Lasso、AIC、BIC）会导致高假阳性率（虚假边），而最小描述长度（MDL）方法无论模型是正确线性假设还是错误线性假设，都能实现正确的邻域选择或更保守的邻域估计（低假阳性率）。此外，研究揭示了岭参数对测试方差的影响导致邻域包含大量边，并与机器学习中的双重下降现象建立了联系。

Conclusion: 在高维网络分析中，要实现恰当的邻域选择，需要将模型空间的体积纳入惩罚项。MDL方法能够克服传统方法在高维下的局限性，提供低假阳性率的保守邻域估计，这为高维网络建模提供了更可靠的理论基础。

Abstract: To avoid missing important variables and their connections in networks, more and more variables are included in network analysis. Here we show that in a setting with many more parameters than observations (high-dimensional) it is possible to get a conservative (i.e., low false positive rate) estimate of the neighbourhood for each node (which connections are in the network). A neighbourhood is often estimated with a linear model, and this leads to two interesting cases: (i) If the true model is linear, then neighbourhood selection work reasonably well, and (ii) if the true model is nonlinear, then neighbourhood selection requires a penalty for the high dimensions. Here we show the impact of the ridge parameter on the mean squared error, and how this leads to low test variance and hence to neighbourhoods with large numbers of edges. We connect these insights with results from machine learning, where the so-called double descent (when more parameters are included than observations, the mean squared error goes down a second time) has put the traditional view on model selection upside down. Essentially, for adequate neighbourhood selection in models with a large number of parameters, the volume of the model space needs to be included in the penalty. Most neighbourhood selection methods (e.g., Lasso, AIC, BIC) lead to spurious edges (high false positive rate), but we prove that in the high-dimensional setting, minimum description length leads to correct neighbourhood selection or smaller (low false positive rates) in both cases when either the model is correctly or incorrectly assumed linear

</details>


### [129] [Sinkhorn Linearization and the Spectral Proxy: Unifying the Statistical and Algorithmic Theory of Feature-Parameterized Inverse Optimal Transport via a Single Spectral Sandwich](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13201)
*Han Dong,Jiaming Li,Yongqiang Gong,Ruixi Li,Yin Liu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了特征参数化代价下的逆最优传输（IOT）的统计与算法理论，核心贡献是Sinkhorn线性化及其谱代理公式，在此基础上建立了可辨识性、稀疏恢复、良置性、收敛性和误设定五个方面的理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆最优传输问题需要在特征参数化代价函数C_theta(i,j)=-theta^T phi(i,j)下进行推断，但缺乏系统的统计与算法理论框架。已有工作对IOT的统计性质、估计量的可辨识性、稀疏恢复以及优化收敛性缺乏严格的刻画，需要建立核心的技术工具来统一推导这些性质。

Method: 本文的核心技术贡献是Sinkhorn线性化，即熵正则化OT计划对代价的隐函数敏感性分析，并提出了一个在谱上精确且在几何上透明的谱代理公式。利用受限Hessian在切空间上的谱三明治不等式（pi_min/epsilon）I <= H_T^{-1} <= (pi_max/epsilon) I，推导出核心界sigma_min >= (pi_min/(a_max epsilon)) sqrt(lambda_min(Sigma))，并在此基础上证明了四个定理（T1可辨识性、T2稀疏一致性、T3良置性、T4收敛性）和一个观察（O5误设定分析）。

Result: 建立了四个主要定理：T1证明theta在规范核的商空间上全局单射，且特征维数界为F <= (K-1)^2；T2证明l1惩罚估计量在不可表示性和得分集中条件下以指数失败概率恢复真实支撑集；T3证明特征矩映射为强单调，逆映射满足Lipschitz常数界L <= epsilon ||Phi^T S_a||_op / (pi_min lambda_min(Sigma))；T4证明局部强凸性保证梯度下降单调收敛。此外O5表明估计量收敛到真实分布的OT模型投影，投影映射的Holder连续性具有随设置变化的经验指数alpha_eff in (0,1)。

Conclusion: 本文为特征参数化代价的逆最优传输问题提供了系统且统一的统计与算法理论框架。Sinkhorn线性化作为核心工具，揭示了IOT估计的谱性质，使得可辨识性、稀疏恢复、良置性与收敛性均可由同一核心界推出，为后续IOT理论研究和算法设计奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: We develop the statistical and algorithmic theory of inverse optimal transport (IOT) under the feature-parameterized cost C_theta(i,j) = -theta^T phi(i,j). The core technical contribution is the Sinkhorn linearization -- the implicit-function sensitivity of the entropic OT plan to the cost -- together with its spectral proxy, a formula that is spectrally exact yet geometrically transparent.
  The restricted Hessian on the tangent space satisfies the spectral sandwich (pi_min/epsilon) I <= H_T^{-1} <= (pi_max/epsilon) I, yielding the single core bound sigma_min >= (pi_min/(a_max epsilon)) sqrt(lambda_min(Sigma)) that drives the entire theory. On this core we establish four theorems and one observation.
  T1 (identifiability): theta is globally injective on the quotient of the gauge kernel, with dimension bound F <= (K-1)^2. T2 (sparsistency): the l1-penalized estimator recovers the true support under irrepresentability and score concentration, with exponential failure probability. T3 (well-posedness): the feature-moment map M(theta) = Phi^T x_theta is strongly monotone, and the inverse is Lipschitz with constant L <= epsilon ||Phi^T S_a||_op / (pi_min lambda_min(Sigma)). T4 (convergence): local strong convexity with mu >= pi_min^2 lambda_min(Sigma) / epsilon^2 guarantees monotone gradient descent convergence. O5 (misspecification): the estimator converges to the OT-model projection of the truth; the Holder continuity of the projection map is assessed numerically, yielding setting-dependent empirical exponents alpha_eff in (0,1).

</details>


### [130] [Wasserstein Filtering: A Sample Selection Method for Robust Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13418)
*Yikai Xu,Zhao Chen,Jian Huang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Wasserstein Filtering（WF），一种基于最优传输的样本选择框架，通过丢弃可疑样本并利用剩余数据估计干净的总体分布，在理论上达到极小极大最优性，并在异常检测和生成建模中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实数据集中，样本常常被污染（包含离群点和局部扰动），而现有的样本选择方法难以同时处理这两类污染，且缺乏理论保证。作者希望提出一种模型无关的预处理工具，能够从被污染的数据中恢复出潜在的干净分布。

Method: WF框架的核心思想是：从候选子集中选择其经验分布与完全污染的经验分布之间Wasserstein距离最大的样本子集，从而优先隔离并移除几何上具有影响力的离群点。为使其计算可行，作者提出三种算法：(1) SinkMarg，一种边际筛选方案；(2) SinkWF，利用熵正则化最优传输的联合优化算法；(3) SlicedWF，基于切片Wasserstein近似的联合优化算法。理论上还引入了FELP（Far Exclusion and Local Projection）污染模型，刻画由良好分离的离群点和局部不可区分扰动构成的污染。

Result: 在FELP污染模型下，作者证明了WF估计器在具有有界协方差的分布族上达到极小极大最优性。大量数值实验覆盖合成数据集、基准异常检测套件和基于扩散模型的鲁棒生成学习，表明WF作为模型无关的预处理工具在离群点检测上表现优异，并且在重度污染下为生成建模提供了显著的下游收益。

Conclusion: Wasserstein Filtering提供了一种实用、高效且具有理论保障的样本筛选方法，能够有效处理数据污染，兼具离群点检测能力和对生成建模的正面促进效应，是一个高度通用的数据预处理工具。

Abstract: Given a dataset where a portion of the samples are contaminated, our goal is to recover the underlying clean population distribution. To this end, we propose Wasserstein Filtering (WF), a novel sample selection framework that discards a fraction of suspicious samples and estimates the target distribution using the empirical measure of the remaining data. The core insight is to select a subset of samples whose empirical distribution maximizes its Wasserstein distance to the fully contaminated empirical distribution, thereby preferentially isolating and removing geometrically influential outliers.
