### [164] [Wave-Assisted Propulsion in Bimodal Sea States: Hydrodynamic Performance and Hydroelastic Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12488)
*Avinash Kumar Pandey,Jung-Hee Seo,Rajat Mittal*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过高保真流固耦合仿真，研究了双峰海况（涌浪与风浪并存）下浸没式扑翼的水动力与水弹性响应。结果表明，尽管双峰波强迫增加了复杂性，推进性能仍遵循在单色波和单峰波中建立的有效峰值频率缩放框架，展示了该框架在海况范围内的鲁棒性。最佳归一化调谐比保持窄范围，但维度扭转弹簧刚度随海况特征变化，凸显了自适应水弹性调谐对最大化推力的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于波辅助推进（WAP）系统的研究主要集中于单色波或单峰波条件，而WAP在真实海洋环境（尤其是涌浪与风浪共存的双峰海况）中的性能尚未得到充分探索，这限制了该技术在实际应用中的设计优化。

Method: 采用高保真流固耦合仿真，对平静、过渡和风暴三种代表性海况下的浸没式扑翼水翼进行分析，水翼通过扭转弹簧提供被动俯仰运动，系统研究双峰海况下的水动力与水弹性响应。

Result: 研究发现：双峰波强迫虽增加复杂性，但推进性能仍遵循与单色波和单峰波相同的有效峰值频率缩放框架；最佳归一化调谐比保持在较窄范围内，而维度扭转弹簧刚度随海况特征显著变化。

Conclusion: WAP系统能够在真实海洋条件下稳健地产生波驱动推力，但需要自适应水弹性调谐以适应不同海况，这为波驱动海洋推进系统的设计提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Wave-assisted propulsion (WAP) systems harvest ocean wave energy to generate propulsive thrust, offering a promising approach for improving endurance and energy efficiency of marine vehicles. Previous studies have focused primarily on monochromatic or unimodal wave conditions, leaving WAP performance in realistic ocean environments largely unexplored. This study investigates the hydrodynamic and hydroelastic response of a submerged flapping hydrofoil operating in bimodal sea states generated by the coexistence of swell and wind-sea wave systems. High-fidelity fluid--structure interaction simulations are performed for representative calm, transitional, and storm conditions, with passive pitching provided through a torsional spring. Simulations show that, despite increased complexity of bimodal wave forcing, propulsion performance follows the same effective peak frequency scaling previously established for monochromatic and unimodal waves, demonstrating the robustness of this scaling framework across a broad range of sea states. The findings further reveal that while the optimal normalized tuning ratio remains within a narrow range, dimensional torsional spring stiffness varies with sea state characteristics, highlighting the need for adaptive hydroelastic tuning to maximize thrust. Overall, the results demonstrate that WAP systems provide a robust means of generating wave-powered thrust under realistic ocean conditions while providing practical guidance for improved design of wave-powered marine propulsion systems.

</details>


### [165] [Dispersion and clustering of deformable droplets in turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12554)
*Yushu Lin,John Palmore*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究了湍流中非球形（变形）液滴的分散特性，采用均匀各向同性湍流（HIT）作为背景流场，探讨液滴变形与惯性之间的相互作用。研究发现变形对液滴分散和聚集的影响依赖于惯性区域：弱惯性液滴变形削弱分散和优先聚集，强惯性液滴变形增强聚集但削弱分散；非惯性液滴因变形产生有效惯性。这一研究表明，准确预测湍流中液滴的分散和聚集必须考虑液滴变形及其非稳态形状振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空工业中喷雾燃烧应用广泛，传统拉格朗日粒子追踪（LPT）方法将喷雾简化为球形离散粒子，忽略了液滴变形对喷雾动力学的影响。已有研究虽指出非球形对液滴蒸发、燃烧和阻力系数的重要性，但仅限于孤立液滴在均匀流中的理想化配置。为在更真实条件下研究液滴变形的影响，本文以HIT为框架研究变形对液滴分散的影响。

Method: 采用均匀各向同性湍流（HIT）作为背景流场，研究不同Stokes数下液滴的变形与分散特性。使用TAB（Taylor Analogy Breakup）模型模拟液滴形状动力学，通过对比完整非稳态TAB模型与其稳态极限结果，验证非稳态形状振荡对统计量的影响。对液滴统计量进行分析以探究变形与惯性之间的相互作用。

Result: 液滴变形对分散和聚集的影响依赖于惯性区域：弱惯性液滴中变形削弱分散和优先浓度（聚集），强惯性液滴中变形增强聚集但削弱分散。要达到相同聚集水平，变形液滴需要更高的Stokes数。非惯性液滴因变形产生有效惯性；完整非稳态TAB模型与稳态极限的对比表明，非稳态形状动力学影响时间相关统计但平均聚集模式不变。

Conclusion: 准确预测湍流喷雾的分散和聚集必须考虑液滴变形及其非稳态形状振荡，仅考虑球形假设的LPT方法存在局限性。

Abstract: Motivated by the application of spray combustion in aviation industry, this work investigates the dispersion of non-spherical droplets in turbulence. The most common strategy for modeling sprays relies on LPT method, which represents the spray as a discrete collection of spherical particles. One limitation of LPT is that it neglects the influence of droplet deformation on spray dynamics. Prior studies have highlighted the importance of non-sphericity in droplet vaporization, combustion and drag coefficient. However, these works are restricted to idealized configurations such as an isolated droplet in a uniform flow. To study droplet deformation in a more realistic configuration, we adopt homogeneous isotropic turbulence (HIT) as the framework to investigate its effect on droplet dispersion. Droplets of various Stokes number are studied to investigate the interplay between deformation and inertia. Analysis of droplet statistics reveals that the impact of droplet deformation on both dispersion and clustering is dependent on the inertia regime. For weakly-inertial droplets, deformation weakens both dispersion and preferential concentration, whereas for strongly-inertial droplets, deformation tends to enhance preferential concentration while weakening dispersion. The results also suggest that to achieve the same level of clustering, deformed droplets require a higher Stokes number. Interestingly, for non-inertial droplets, the deformation seems to induce an effective inertia. This is verified by a comparison between the full unsteady TAB model and its steady-state limit, which suggests that unsteady shape dynamics affect temporal correlation statistics, but leave the mean clustering pattern unchanged. These findings demonstrate that accounting for droplet deformation and its unsteady shape oscillation is essential for accurately predicting droplet dispersion and clustering in turbulence.

</details>


### [166] [Infrared imaging of thermally-driven jets and eddies in planetary-style laboratory turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12694)
*Cy S. David,Remy Monville,Daphne Lemasquerier,Lvcian Vltava,Jonathan M. Aurnou*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 这是关于2025年美国物理学会流体动力学分会（DFD）Milton van Dyke奖获奖海报的说明性摘要，该奖项颁发给在DFD流体运动画廊展示的工作，海报可在线上获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该摘要旨在告知读者这篇论文与2025年美国物理学会DFD分会Milton van Dyke奖获奖海报相关，并引导读者访问在线画廊获取原始海报内容。

Method: 该摘要属于会议获奖海报的说明性条目，实质内容需通过提供的DOI链接访问Gallery of Fluid Motion获取原始海报。

Result: 该作品荣获2025年度Milton van Dyke奖，该奖项认可其在DFD流体运动画廊中的出色展示。

Conclusion: 该摘要提供获奖作品的元信息与访问指引，具体的流体动力学研究成果需查阅原始海报获取。

Abstract: This paper is associated with a poster winner of a 2025 American Physical Society's Division of Fluid Dynamics (DFD) Milton van Dyke Award for work presented at the DFD Gallery of Fluid Motion. The original poster is available online at the Gallery of Fluid Motion, at https://doi.org/10.1103/APS.DFD.2025.GFM.P012

</details>


### [167] [Bayesian optimization and topographic exploration of drag-reducing dimples for aerodynamic surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12826)
*Sangjoon Lee,M. Erden Yildizdag,Haris M. Sheikh*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究将凹坑形貌设计视为一个四维混合变量优化问题，采用贝叶斯优化器MixMOBO结合浸没边界大涡模拟，在摩擦雷诺数180的槽道流中系统地探索了凹坑的设计空间，发现一种较深、完全填充、流向拉长的菱形凹坑可实现13.2%的减阻，显著高于以往报道值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凹坑虽然有望降低气动表面阻力，但其浅凹几何能否带来净收益高度依赖于形貌细节，而完整的凹坑设计空间尚未得到系统性的映射与探索，缺乏对设计变量的综合优化研究。

Method: 将凹坑设计建模为包含凹坑类型、深度、面内尺度、流向拉伸比四个变量的混合变量优化问题，使用混合变量贝叶斯优化器MixMOBO进行设计空间探索，并以浸没边界大涡模拟求解恒定流量下Friction Reynolds数为180的槽道流作为评估手段。

Result: 优化得到的最优解为一种较深、完全填充、流向拉长的菱形凹坑，实现13.2%的减阻率，明显超过以往报告的数值。高斯过程元模型敏感性分析显示凹坑拓扑是主导因素，覆盖率与拉伸主要通过交互作用产生影响，深度本身没有普适的符号性影响。近壁流动分析表明，优秀设计对应完全附着、沟槽状的流动，而较差设计倾向产生局部流动分离并带来不利的压差阻力。

Conclusion: 研究提供了减阻凹坑的关键设计见解，指出凹坑拓扑形状是决定减阻性能的首要因素，并揭示了优秀设计与附着沟槽流动、差设计与流动分离之间的机理联系，为后续凹坑减阻设计提供了系统性指导。

Abstract: Dimples offer a promising route to reducing drag on aerodynamic surfaces. However, whether such shallow concavities yield a net benefit depends sensitively on their topography, which demands systematic mapping and exploration of their comprehensive design space. In this study, dimple design is examined as a mixed-variable optimization over four design variables: dimple type, depth, in-plane scale, and streamwise stretch. The design space is explored using MixMOBO, a Bayesian optimizer, coupled with immersed-boundary large eddy simulations of channel flows at a constant flow rate corresponding to a flat channel at a friction Reynolds number of 180. The optimal solution, a relatively deep, fully packed, streamwise-elongated diamond dimple, attains a 13.2% drag reduction, notably above previously reported values. A Gaussian process metamodel sensitivity analysis identifies dimple topology as the dominant factor, with coverage and elongation acting mainly through interactions, and depth itself carrying no universal sign. A near-wall flow analysis links the leading designs to fully attached, groove-like flow, whereas poorer designs tend to produce local flow separation that incurs adverse form drag. From these findings, key design insights for drag-reducing dimples are provided.

</details>


### [168] [Controlling the dynamics of an electric-field-driven droplet on a lubricant-infused micropillar surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12868)
*Geng Wang,Junyu Yang,Timan Lei,Jin Chen,Halim Kusumaatmaja,Kai Li,Kai H. Luo*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种格子玻尔兹曼方案，完整捕捉界面流动与电流体动力学之间的相互作用，用于研究微柱状润滑液注入表面（LIS）上电场驱动液滴的动力学。通过结合模拟与解析计算，建立了液滴运动时拖曳力和电力的定量表达式，并证明模型能准确捕捉可编程操控中的液滴动力学（包括周期运动和长距离输运）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电场作为一种非接触控制手段可用于驱动润滑液注入表面上的液滴动力学，在药物制造到3D打印等领域具有广泛前景。然而，由于LIS上存在多种可能的液滴润滑状态，电场驱动下的液滴动力学仍缺乏深入理解，需要建立可靠的物理模型来取代试错式操控。

Method: 开发了一种格子玻尔兹曼数值方案，完整耦合界面流动与电流体动力学；通过数值模拟与解析计算相结合，定量推导出运动液滴所受拖曳力和电力的表达式。

Result: 建立了拖曳力与电力的定量解析表达式，并验证了所提模型能准确捕捉可编程操控过程中的液滴动力学行为，包括周期性运动和长距离输运。

Conclusion: 所建立的可依赖理论模型有望消除对试错实验的依赖，从而彻底变革液滴动力学的精确控制方式。

Abstract: As a non-contact control approach, electric field (EF) can be utilised to drive droplet dynamics on a lubricant-infused surface (LIS), with numerous potential applications ranging from drug manufacturing to 3D printing. However, the resulting droplet dynamics remain poorly understood, especially as there are several possible droplet lubrication states on LIS. Here, we develop a lattice Boltzmann scheme that fully captures the interplay between the interfacial flows and electrohydrodynamics and harness it to investigate EF driven droplets on micropillar LIS. Combining simulations and analytical calculations, we establish quantitative expressions for the drag force and the electric force acting on a moving droplet. We demonstrate that the models can accurately capture droplet dynamics during programmable manipulation, including periodic motion and long-distance transport. Such reliable theoretical models can potentially transform precision control of droplet dynamics by removing the reliance on trial and error tests.

</details>


### [169] [Second-order stochastic modeling of particle resuspension: macroscopic degeneracy and anomalous pre-detachment transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12941)
*David Ben-Shlomo,Ronen Berkovich,Eyal Fattal*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出一个增强角速度状态、引入有限相关切向加速度的二阶马尔可夫拉格朗日随机模型，用于粒子再悬浮模拟。虽然该模型与一阶公式在宏观再悬浮分数上几乎一致（表现出宏观简并性），但多尺度轨迹统计能打破这种简并：加速度增强公式改变了短时正则性（S2(τ)∝τ²）、维持角速度相关性、产生更强方向不对称性和更重的增量尾部，并揭示雷诺数依赖的异常输运窗口。这些结果表明多尺度轨迹统计是区分随机再悬浮模型的关键判别量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子再悬浮模型通常仅用宏观再悬浮分数来评估，但这一集成可观测量可能掩盖导致脱离的时域动力学细节。为了揭示微观脱离过程中的动力学信息，需要发展能够区分不同随机描述形式的建模框架和统计判别方法。

Method: 作者发展了一个二阶马尔可夫拉格朗日随机模型，通过引入有限相关的切向加速度来增强角速度状态。该模型在湍流通道流（Reτ∈[60,430]）中进行检验，并与已建立的一阶模型进行比较。分析涉及多尺度轨迹统计量，包括二阶结构函数、角速度相关性、增量分布形态、方向不对称性以及幸存者条件均方位移等。

Result: 两个模型在宏观再悬浮分数上几乎重叠，揭示了宏观简并性；但多尺度轨迹统计可打破这种简并。加速度增强模型使短时正则性从S2∝τ变为S2∝τ²，维持了角速度相关性，产生更强的方向不对称性和更重的增量尾部。幸存者条件均方位移暴露了雷诺数依赖的异常输运窗口（近似弹道甚至超弹道标度）。在Reτ≈60时，轨迹进入独特的低雷诺数统计状态，表现为速度和加速度去相关收敛以及近高斯增量。

Conclusion: 多尺度轨迹统计是区分不同随机再悬浮模型的基本判别工具；有限加速度相关性提供了超出宏观脱离动力学的额外动力学信息源。宏观再悬浮分数存在简并性，仅用它评估模型会导致对潜在物理过程的不可区分性。

Abstract: Particle resuspension models are commonly evaluated using the macroscopic resuspended fraction, although this integrated observable may conceal the temporal dynamics leading to detachment. Here, a second-order Markovian Lagrangian stochastic model is developed by augmenting the angular-velocity state with a finite-correlated tangential acceleration. The model is examined over turbulent channel flows with ($Re_τ\in [60, 430]$) and compared with an established first-order formulation. The two models produce nearly overlapping resuspended fractions, revealing a macroscopic degeneracy between distinct stochastic descriptions. Multiscale trajectory statistics break this degeneracy. The acceleration-augmented formulation changes the short-time regularity from $S_2(τ) \propto τ$ to $S_2(τ) \propto τ^2$, sustains angular-velocity correlation, and produces stronger directional asymmetry and heavier increment tails. The survivor-conditioned mean square displacement exposes a Reynolds-number-dependent anomalous-transport window, in which the attached-particle ensemble grows more rapidly than the diffusive reference and locally approaches ballistic and super-ballistic scaling before crossing toward diffusion-like transport. This finite-time pathway records how particles approach detachment and is compressed out of the macroscopic resuspended fraction. At $Re_τ\approx 60$, the trajectories enter a distinct low-Reynolds-number statistical state characterized by converging velocity and acceleration decorrelation and near-Gaussian increments. These results establish multiscale trajectory statistics as essential discriminants between stochastic resuspension models and identify finite acceleration correlation as a source of dynamical information beyond macroscopic detachment kinetics.

</details>


### [170] [Intermittent Vortex Merging and Extreme Drag in Transitional Airfoil Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13398)
*Shishir Gautam,Chitrarth Prasad*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过二维直接数值模拟研究了过渡翼型流动中罕见的剧烈阻力偏离事件，揭示了这些极端事件源于二次涡量喷发导致的涡团聚集释放，而非单个涡的标称脱落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kármán涡街脱落过程中的间歇性偏离可产生罕见且显著的阻力突变，对过渡翼型流动的空气动力学性能产生重要影响。理解这些极端事件的产生机制对于预测和缓解极端气动载荷至关重要。

Method: 采用二维直接数值模拟（DNS）研究NACA0012翼型在迎角5°、来流马赫数0.4、弦长雷诺数为5×10^4和5×10^5下的流动。通过事件分辨分析追踪主涡释放和二次涡量喷发的动力学过程。

Result: 低雷诺数下，主涡通过壁面产生的反向二次涡量喷发而从分离剪切层释放。极端事件源于短时间内多次二次涡量喷发形成的涡团聚集释放，差分对流进一步缩小涡间距并引发近尾缘强相互作用（变形、丝化或核心合并），产生局部吸力峰和阻力骤增。高雷诺数下也观察到相似的紧凑涡组织和近尾缘相互作用。

Conclusion: 控制二次涡量动力学以调节涡释放时机，可能成为破坏涡团聚集释放、缓解极端气动载荷的有效途径。

Abstract: Intermittent departures from nominal Kelvin--Helmholtz shedding can produce rare and pronounced drag excursions in transitional airfoil flow. We examine these events using two-dimensional direct numerical simulations of flow over a NACA0012 airfoil at an angle of attack of $5^\circ$, a freestream Mach number of $0.4$, and chord-based Reynolds numbers of $5\times10^4$ and $5\times10^5$. At the lower Reynolds number, event-resolved analysis shows that individual primary vortices are released from the separated shear layer through the eruption of wall-generated, opposite-signed secondary vorticity. Each eruption interrupts the connection between a developing primary vortex and its feeding shear layer, releasing the vortex downstream. During nominal shedding, the vortex reaching the trailing-edge region is associated with a single such release and remains sufficiently isolated to pass the trailing edge without strong collective interaction. Extreme events instead arise through clustered vortex release, in which several secondary-vorticity eruptions occur within a short interval and produce a compact group of primary vortices with small initial streamwise spacing. Differential convection further reduces their spacing and promotes strong near-trailing-edge interactions, where the combined pressure footprint of these vortices produces a localized suction peak and a sharp increase in drag. These interactions range from prolonged deformation and filamentation to rapid core coalescence. Similar compact vortex organization and near-trailing-edge interactions are recovered at $Re=5\times10^5$, indicating that the downstream event pathway persists despite the smaller vortical scales.These findings suggest that controlling vortex-release timing through secondary-vorticity dynamics may provide a route to disrupt clustered release and mitigate extreme aerodynamic loading.

</details>


### [171] [Modern aerodynamics models do not capture important unsteady forces--the failure of the quasi-steady approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13424)
*Victoria M. Malarczyk,Marcus Hultmark*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过高加压风洞实验，评估了准稳态近似在风力机叶片非定常空气动力学中的有效性，发现该近似在通常应用的频率范围内并不成立，导致传统模型会显著低估叶片载荷，尤其在接近失速条件下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代风力机转子直径已超过200米，运行在缺乏经验数据和模型验证的条件下。准稳态近似常用于简化非定常气动响应的建模，但其在不同频率范围内的有效性尚未得到充分验证，可能低估叶片载荷。

Method: 使用高加压风洞模拟大型风力机叶片所处的实际运行条件，系统性地评估准稳态近似在慢变化来流条件下对气动响应的预测精度，覆盖通常应用该近似的频率范围。

Result: 发现准稳态近似在其通常适用的大范围频率区间内并不成立，尤其在接近失速条件下，传统模型会显著低估风力机叶片的载荷。

Conclusion: 现有准稳态近似模型不足以准确预测大型风力机叶片的气动载荷，特别是在失速状态下，需要开发考虑非定常效应的新模型以保证设计和运行安全。

Abstract: Aerodynamic unsteadiness is inherent to the operation of many engineering applications, especially those that involve large-scale rotating blades, such as modern wind turbines. Over the past few decades, wind turbine rotors have grown rapidly and are now exceeding 200 meters in diameter, causing them to operate in conditions where limited empirical data are available, and where models have not been validated. Here, we use a highly pressurized wind tunnel to probe these conditions and evaluate the quasi-steady approximation, often used to simplify the modeling of the unsteady aerodynamic response of an airfoil to slow changes in inflow conditions. We find that the quasi-steady approximation is not valid for a large range of frequencies where it is normally applied. This finding suggests that the loading on wind turbine blades will be significantly underestimated when using conventional models, especially near stalling conditions, which modern wind turbines typically encounter.

</details>


### [172] [Lattice Boltzmann Method for Compressible Navier-Stokes-Fourier Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13437)
*Fedor Bukreev,Adrian Kummerländer,Mathias J. Krause*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由符号编译器自动生成的、针对三维可压缩Navier-Stokes-Fourier方程的格子Boltzmann格式，在D3Q7格子上以单精度离散，粘性应力与热流作为输运状态量。通过与Sod和Becker精确解对比，激波厚度收敛阶为一阶；在马赫数1.25的超声速Taylor-Green涡算例中，512³网格下其膨胀耗散精度比七个对比求解器中表现最佳者还要高三十个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上可压缩格子Boltzmann方法在三维可压缩Navier-Stokes-Fourier方程上的数值精度与可靠性不足，尤其是粘性应力与热流的处理以及构造成本。本文追求一种自动生成的、严格由声明系统推导的高保真度求解器，以提升超声速流动中耗散性质的预测精度。

Method: 采用符号编译器从声明的三维可压缩Navier-Stokes-Fourier方程组自动推导格子Boltzmann格式，将粘性应力与热通量作为输运状态量进行离散，采用D3Q7格子和单精度浮点运算。所有算子（碰撞、本构闭包及激波传感器）均采用局部单点读取，仅通过沿格子特征线的流式传输与邻域通信，确保严格局部性与格子Boltzmann流式结构。

Result: 与Sod和Becker精确解对比，激波厚度以第一阶收敛。在M0=1.25的超声速Taylor-Green涡上，512³网格的膨胀耗散结果与参考值吻合度超过七个对比求解器，比次优者精度高30%。

Conclusion: 该自动编译生成的格子Boltzmann方案在可压缩超声速流动中展现出优于多种现有求解器的耗散精度，同时保持完全的局部运算与流式通信结构，验证了符号编译器驱动的自动格式推导在可压缩格子Boltzmann方法中的有效性与潜力。

Abstract: A lattice Boltzmann scheme for the three-dimensional compressible Navier--Stokes--Fourier equations, derived automatically from the declared system by a symbolic compiler, is validated against exact solutions and published reference data. The declared system carries the viscous stress and the heat flux as transported state, and is discretized on a D3Q7 lattice in single precision. Against the exact Sod and Becker solutions the captured shock thickness converges at first order. On the supersonic Taylor-Green vortex at $M_0 = 1.25$ the scheme at $512^3$ matches the reference dilatational dissipation more closely than seven compared solvers, by thirty percent over the next best. Every operator in this solver, the generated collision and constitutive closure and the added shock sensor alike, reads only the cell it acts on, and data reaches a neighbor only by streaming along the lattice characteristics.

</details>


### [173] [Air-water cavity in multi-plunging jet impacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13479)
*Narendra Dev,Hèléne Scolan,J. John Soundar Jerome,Jean-Philippe Matas*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种新型的圆顶状气-水空腔，由紧密排列的垂直水射流撞击液面形成（类似淋浴喷头构型）。通过高速成像、激光诱导荧光和光学相检测探针，发现空腔由多个空气茎和薄片组成，会拉伸、回缩并截断产生气泡，涵盖从几微米到约5毫米的宽范围气泡尺寸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多射流撞击液面形成的空腔结构及其气泡产生机制，填补关于紧密排列射流组（如淋浴头构型）空腔形成机理的研究空白。

Method: 实验研究：使用同心圆环排列的圆形射流构成喷头；采用高速成像、激光诱导荧光（LIF）和光学相检测探针进行观测；改变冲击速度、射流数量和射流间距，测量空腔尺寸。

Result: 空腔由多个空气茎和薄片构成，经历拉伸、回缩和截断过程产生气泡；揭示了多种气泡产生机制以及气泡云中的聚并和破碎过程；气泡尺寸范围从几微米到约5毫米；空腔尺寸随冲击速度、射流数量和间距变化。

Conclusion: 这种独特的气-水空腔是由连续环状垂直射流撞击液池时逐步与池水混合所产生的液体夹带形成的。该发现有助于理解复杂多射流系统中的气液相互作用和气泡生成机制。

Abstract: We report the formation of an original dome-shaped air-water cavity at the impact site of closely-packed plunging water jets. The injector assembly consists of concentric rings of circular jets, similar to a shower-head configuration. We infer from high-speed imaging, LASER-induced fluorescence, and optical phase detection probes that the cavity consists of multiple stems and sheets of air that stretch, retract and pinch off to produce bubbles. We also illustrate various bubble production mechanisms in the cavity, along with coalescence and bubble breakup scenarios in the subsequent bubble cloud. Thereby, we describe the wide range of bubble sizes generated by such jets, from a few micrometers up to about $5$~mm. Measurements of the cavity size are carried out for varying impact velocity, number of jets, and jet spacing. We propose that this distinctive air-water cavity is generated by liquid entrainment when successive rings of plunging jets progressively mix with the pool water.

</details>


### [174] [DD-RNO: A Domain-Decomposed Routed Neural Operator for Airfoil Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13490)
*T. A. Mehta,P. S. Bhati,H. D. Akolekar*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出DD-RNO（域分解路由神经算子），结合谱几何编码器与两项物理驱动创新——可微域路由机制和学习的规范求积（LCQ），解决了深度学习代理在翼型RANS流场预测中的两大瓶颈（近壁边界层与远场势流分辨率冲突、压力积分数值不稳定）。在AirfRANS基准上，速度场MSE较最强基线降低12-17倍（域外雷诺数外推下达23倍），阻力MSE降低7.5倍，阻力秩相关系数从0.250提升至0.997，推理速度较传统RANS求解器快10000倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习替代RANS求解器用于翼型流场预测面临两个持续瓶颈：单一神经架构无法同时解析近壁尖锐边界层与远场光滑势流；连续场逼近下计算壁面法向速度梯度存在数值不稳定，导致力预测精度受损。这两个问题阻碍了神经代理在实时气动设计与优化中的应用。

Method: DD-RNO包含三个核心组件：（1）谱几何编码器用于编码翼型几何；（2）可微域路由机制，将流场划分为无粘区、边界层和尾迹区，将查询点分发到专用的区域解码器；（3）学习规范求积（LCQ），以流动条件相关的学习积分权重替代不稳定的压力积分，直接从表面压力预测升力和阻力。

Result: 在AirfRANS基准上：速度场MSE降低12倍（ux）和17倍（uy），超最强基线；域外雷诺数外推下达23倍改进，证明路由机制基于物理泛化而非过拟合训练分布。LCQ使阻力MSE降低7.5倍，阻力秩相关系数从0.250提升至0.997。消融实验显示移除域路由使速度误差增加8.2倍，移除LCQ使阻力相对误差增加40倍以上。推理速度约144毫秒/样本，较传统RANS加速10000倍。

Conclusion: DD-RNO证明了物理驱动的架构设计（域路由与学习积分）对神经代理性能的关键作用，在精度和速度上均满足实时气动设计与优化循环的需求。两个组件均不可缺失，且域外泛化能力表明方法编码了物理规律而非仅拟合训练数据。

Abstract: Deep learning surrogates for RANS flow prediction around airfoils face two persistent bottlenecks. A single neural architecture cannot simultaneously resolve sharp near-wall boundary layers and smooth far-field potential flow. Additionally, force prediction is undermined by the numerical instability of computing wall-normal velocity gradients from continuous-field approximations.
  Both of these points are addressed with a DD-RNO (domain-decomposed routed neural operator), combining a spectral geometry encoder with two physics-guided innovations: (a) a differentiable domain routing mechanism that partitions the flow field into inviscid, boundary-layer, and wake regimes---dispatching query points to specialized regional decoders, and (b) learned canonical quadrature (LCQ), which replaces unstable pressure integration with flow-conditioned, learned integration weights that predict lift and drag directly from surface pressure.
  On the AirfRANS benchmark, DD-RNO cuts velocity field mean-square error (MSE) by 17$x$ ($u_x$) and 12$x$ ($u_y$) over the strongest baseline, widening to 23$x$ under out-of-distribution Reynolds extrapolation---evidence that the routing mechanism generalizes with the physics it encodes rather than merely fitting the training distribution. LCQ reduces drag MSE by 7.5$x$ relative to conventional pressure integration and raises drag rank correlation from $ρ= 0.250$ to $ρ= 0.997$. Ablations confirm that both components are indispensable to performance: removing domain routing increases velocity error by 8.2$x$, and removing LCQ increases relative drag error more than 40-fold. At ~144 ms per sample---a 10,000$x$ speedup over conventional RANS solvers---DD-RNO offers a surrogate accurate and fast enough for real-time aerodynamic design and optimization loops.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [TCAD Cryogenic Model Parameters Calibration based on 65 nm NMOS Experimental Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12692)
*Wesley Xi,Vikram Magendar,Alhaji A Sharka,Hiu Yung Wong*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对65nm n型MOSFET在292K至4.5K低温范围内的器件建模进行研究。通过流片实测、TCAD参数校准以及TEM表征晶体管尺寸，实现了不同温度下CV、IDVG和IDVD曲线的良好拟合。校准的核心模型包括Lucent模型（扩展PhuMob、Lombardi表面迁移率和Hänsch高场迁移率模型）以及调整带宽的带尾模型。研究发现除饱和速度外无需新增温度相关模型，校准方法具有跨技术和跨器件类型的通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低温CMOS器件建模是量子计算等极低温应用的关键基础，但现有TCAD模型在深低温（如4.5K）下的准确性不足，需要系统性的参数校准方法和拟合策略来确保仿真与实测吻合。

Method: 首先在292K至4.5K温度范围内对65nm nMOSFET进行流片和电学测量；利用TEM确定晶体管真实尺寸；随后通过TCAD校准将仿真曲线与实测CV、IDVG、IDVD曲线拟合。校准的模型包括Lucent模型（含扩展PhuMob迁移率模型、Lombardi表面迁移率模型和Hänsch高场迁移率模型），以及用于拟合各温度亚阈值摆幅的带宽调整带尾模型。

Result: 校准后的TCAD参数在292K至4.5K全温度范围内使CV、IDVG和IDVD仿真曲线与实验曲线良好吻合。除饱和速度外，未发现需要新增其他温度相关模型。校准的模型参数不依赖特定工艺技术，具有一定的通用性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了基于TCAD的低温晶体管建模方法可行且有效，校准参数可迁移至其他技术节点，校准流程和拟合策略也可推广用于其他类型晶体管的低温建模，为极低温电子器件仿真提供了可靠参考。

Abstract: In this paper, 65 nm n-type Metal-Oxide-Semiconductor Field-Effect-Transistors (nMOSFETs) are taped out and measured at 292 K to 4.5 K. Technology Computer-Aided-Design (TCAD) model parameters are then calibrated to fit the simulation curves to experimental electrical curves. Transmission electron microscopy (TEM) is used to unveil the dimensions of the transistors to ensure accurate modeling. The experimental and simulation capacitance-voltage (CV) curves, drain current - gate voltage (IDVG) curves, and drain current - drain voltage (IDVD) curves are fitted well using the calibrated parameters. The major models calibrated are the Lucent model, which is comprised of an extended PhuMob model, Lombardi model for surface mobility, and the Hänsch high field mobility model. A band-tail model with adjusted band width is applied to fit the sub-threshold swings at all temperatures. The calibrated parameters are expected to be applicable to other technologies because TCAD models are not technology-specific. It is also found that, except for saturation velocity, no new temperature-dependent model is needed. The calibration process and fitting strategies are detailed and are expected to be applicable to calibrate other transistor types.

</details>


### [176] [A discrete duality finite volume method with harmonic average for semiconductor drift-diffusion equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12808)
*Shuya Liu,Zhicheng Liu,Bo Lin,Chijie Zhuang,Qingyuan Shi,Weizhu Bao,Rong Zeng*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将调和平均稳定化与离散对偶有限体积法相结合的新方案（DDFV-HA），用于半导体器件中的稳态漂移-扩散模型模拟。与经典有限体积Scharfetter-Gummel（FVSG）方法相比，DDFV-HA在高品质网格上与FVSG性能相当，但在低品质或畸变网格上更可靠、更精确，并通过真实晶闸管模拟验证了其在复杂不规则域中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的FVSG方法依赖Voronoi图，在高质量Delaunay网格上表现良好，但在不规则或畸变网格上效果不佳，限制了半导体模拟在复杂不规则域中的适用性，需要一种不受网格质量限制的新方法。

Method: 将调和平均稳定化（harmonic average stabilization）集成到离散对偶有限体积法（DDFV）中，形成DDFV-HA方案，以克服对Voronoi网格拓扑的依赖，提高对低质量网格的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验表明DDFV-HA在高品质网格上与FVSG结果吻合，在低品质网格上比FVSG更可靠、更精确；在真实晶闸管器件上的应用进一步验证了其在复杂不规则域上的有效性。

Conclusion: DDFV-HA是一种适用于半导体漂移-扩散模拟的稳健数值方案，特别适合高质量网格难以生成的复杂不规则几何域。

Abstract: The stationary drift-diffusion model is widely used to model charge transport in semiconductor devices. Classical methods, such as the finite volume Scharfetter--Gummel (FVSG) method, perform well on high-quality Delaunay meshes but struggle on irregular or distorted meshes due to their reliance on Voronoi diagrams. To overcome this mesh limitation, this article introduces a new approach that integrates harmonic average stabilization into the discrete duality finite volume method (DDFV-HA). To validate our scheme, we compare DDFV-HA and FVSG for semiconductor simulations on both high- and low-quality meshes. Experiments show that DDFV-HA matches FVSG on high-quality meshes and is more reliable and accurate on low-quality meshes. Applying DDFV-HA to a real-world thyristor further confirms that it is well-suited for semiconductor simulations in complex, irregular domains where high-quality meshes are not easy to generate.

</details>


### [177] [Using Diffusion Models to Estimate Uncertainties in Analytic Continuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13123)
*Sagi Meir,Daniel Freedman,Barak Hirshberg*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于扩散模型的生成式框架，用于解决解析延拓这一病态逆问题。与传统的回归式神经网络方法只输出单个解不同，该框架学习与给定虚时关联函数（iTCF）一致的功率谱分布，从而量化不确定性并评估反问题的固有难度（通过新提出的"不确定性伪体积"指标）。在液态仲氢的路径积分分子动力学模拟数据上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析延拓（将虚时关联函数通过逆拉普拉斯变换重建实频域谱）是典型的病态逆问题，存在大量谱与同一iTCF匹配。现有的回归式神经网络方法（包括最大熵方法）只输出单一解，本质上是真实解空间的平均值，无法捕捉合理功率谱的完整分布，因此无法量化结果的不确定性。

Method: 提出一个基于扩散模型（diffusion-based）的生成式分析框架：该框架学习与给定iTCF一致的谱分布，而非单一映射。通过分析该分布的扩散程度（spread）和结构，引入新的指标——不确定性伪体积（uncertainty pseudo-volume）来量化每个反演问题的内在难度。将该框架应用于液态仲氢的路径积分分子动力学模拟产生的iTCF数据。

Result: 在液态仲氢的iTCF数据上，该方法成功获得了带误差棒的自扩散系数，并识别出一个次要高频峰可能是虚假伪影。相比此前的不确定性量化尝试，该生成式方法建立在具体的概率基础之上，提供了理论上更严谨的置信度度量。

Conclusion: 生成式扩散框架为解析延拓问题提供了扎实的概率论基础，不仅能够量化重建谱的不确定性，还能通过不确定性伪体积这一指标定量评估每个反演问题的固有难度，在理论和实践层面均优于以往的回归式方法。

Abstract: Inverse problems are ubiquitous in physics, chemistry, and engineering, arising when reconstructing hidden quantities from indirect measurements. A key example is the analytic continuation of imaginary-time correlation functions (iTCFs) to the real-frequency domain. This process requires an inverse Laplace transform, which is inherently ill-posed and highly sensitive to small input variations. Recent neural network (NN)-based methods have shown promising results by learning mappings from imaginary-time to real-frequency spectra, often outperforming traditional techniques such as maximum entropy. However, because the problem is ill-posed, many spectra fit the same iTCF. Regression-based approaches output a single solution, which approximates an average over the true solution space, and therefore fail to capture the full distribution of plausible power spectra. To address this issue, we introduce a diffusion-based framework for analytic continuation that learns the distribution of spectra consistent with a given iTCF. It offers two key advantages. First, it quantifies uncertainty directly from the learned distribution. Second, by analyzing the spread and structure of this distribution, we can quantitatively assess the intrinsic hardness of each inversion problem. We measure this hardness with a new metric, the uncertainty pseudo-volume. Applying the framework to an iTCF from a path-integral molecular dynamics simulation of liquid parahydrogen, we obtain the self-diffusion coefficient with an error bar and flag a secondary high-frequency peak as a possible spurious artifact. In contrast to previous attempts at uncertainty quantification, our generative approach rests on a concrete probabilistic basis, providing a more theoretically grounded measure of confidence in the reconstructed power spectra.

</details>


### [178] [Modeling Bond-Dependent Kitaev-like interaction in 2D Edge-Sharing Tetrahedral Magnets: FeX (X=Te, Se)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13427)
*Mengdong Li,Can Huangb,Bingjie Liu,Zhixin Liu,Yanfei Pan,Jiyu Fan,Chunlan Ma,Daning Shi,Yan Zhua*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算，在单层FeTe和FeSe中构建了Kitaev-like模型，首次证实了边共享四面体配位环境中存在键依赖的Ising型磁相互作用（由硫族元素介导的自旋轨道耦合与四面体晶体场共同诱导），并揭示了该相互作用在不同材料中与单离子各向异性的竞争关系，为理解铁基超导母体化合物中的磁无序提供了微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kitaev模型等键依赖磁相互作用此前被认为仅在边共享八面体配位体系中存在，而在边共享四面体环境中（如铁基超导母体化合物）能否存在类似相互作用仍是一个悬而未决的问题。作者旨在回答这一开放问题，扩展Kitaev物理的适用范围。

Method: 1) 为单层FeTe和FeSe构造Kitaev-like模型；2) 通过第一性原理计算得到磁各向异性能图；3) 将计算映射到具有代表性的线性磁序上；4) 从总磁各向异性能中解耦出键依赖相互作用与单离子各向异性的贡献。

Result: 发现由硫族元素介导的自旋轨道耦合与四面体晶体场几何共同诱导的键依赖Ising型相互作用。在FeTe中该Kitaev-like相互作用主导磁各向异性；在FeSe中它与符号相反的单离子各向异性激烈竞争，产生非共线局域各向异性轴并导致固有的单自旋位阻挫，提供超越各向同性交换模型之外的磁无序微观机制。

Conclusion: 边共享四面体磁体被确立为键依赖相互作用的新载体，Kitaev物理的适用范围从八面体配位拓展到了四面体配位体系，对铁基超导母体化合物的磁性起源理解具有重要启示。

Abstract: Bond-dependent magnetic interactions, exemplified by the Kitaev model, are known to arise from the interplay between spin-orbit coupling(SOC) and specific coordination geometries, yet their existence has so far been predominantly associated with edge-sharing octahedral systems. Whether analogous interactions survive in edge- sharing tetrahedral environments - relevant to iron-based superconductor parent compounds - remains an open question. Here, we construct a Kitaev-like model for monolayer FeTe and FeSe and demonstrate the presence of a previously unrecognized bond-dependent Ising-type interaction, induced jointly by chalcogen-mediated SOC and the tetrahedral crystal-field geometry. By mapping the first-principles calculations derived magnetic anisotropy energy mapping across representative linear magnetic orders, we disentangle the bond-dependent contributions from single-ion anisotropy. We reveal that the Kitaev-like interaction dominates the magnetic anisotropy in FeTe, whereas in FeSe, it fiercely competes with a single-ion anisotropy of opposite sign. The resulting noncollinear local anisotropy axes generate intrinsic single-site spin frustration, providing a microscopic mechanism for magnetic disorder beyond isotropic exchange models. Our results establish edge-sharing tetrahedral magnets as a new setting for bond-dependent interactions and extend the scope of Kitaev physics beyond octahedral coordination.

</details>


### [179] [Characteristic Mode Analysis of Plasmonic Nanostructures Using Hydrodynamic Volume Integral Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13435)
*Meruyert Khamitova,Ran Zhao,Doolos Aibek Uulu,Sebastian Celis Sierra,Hakan Bagci*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将特征模式分析（CMA）扩展至等离激元纳米结构的方法。通过将流体动力学模型与体积分方程耦合，推导出单一流体动力学体积分方程，并转化为广义特征值问题，实现对金属纳米结构固有共振的无激励依赖模态分析。在纳米球、纳米棒和纳米二聚体三种结构上验证，该方法能识别局部模型无法捕捉的由非局域响应引起的额外共振。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属纳米结构在亚波长尺度下具有等离激元纳米天线特性，但其响应呈现非局域性，流体动力学模型被广泛采用。然而现有求解器仅能计算给定激励下的响应，无法直接揭示结构的固有共振模式，缺乏激励无关的模态分析方法。

Method: 将耦合的流体动力学方程与体积分方程简化为仅含感应电流的单一流体动力学体积分方程（HVIE），对该方程进行离散化，并置于特征模式分析（CMA）框架中构造广义特征值问题。求解得到特征模式电流和模态显著性曲线，从而识别结构的固有共振。

Result: 在纳米球、纳米棒和纳米二聚体三种金属纳米结构上进行了验证。结果表明该方法能够识别各结构的固有共振，包括给定源未激励出的共振模式，以及局部模型（如常规金属模型）中不存在的、由非局域响应引起的额外共振。

Conclusion: 该框架为等离激元纳米结构的模态机制提供了物理洞察，可作为其分析与设计的实用工具。

Abstract: Metallic nanostructures confine electromagnetic fields at subwavelength scales, making them attractive as plasmonic nanoantennas. At these scales, the response of metals becomes nonlocal, and the hydrodynamic model is widely used to capture this response. However, existing solvers provide only the response to a prescribed excitation and do not directly reveal the intrinsic resonances of the structure. This work extends the characteristic mode analysis to plasmonic nanostructures to enable excitation-independent modal analysis of their resonant behavior. For simple metals, the coupled hydrodynamic and volume integral equations are reduced to a single hydrodynamic volume integral equation in terms of the induced current. The equation is discretized and cast as a generalized eigenvalue problem within the characteristic mode analysis framework, whose solution yields the characteristic mode currents and modal significance curves of the structure. The proposed framework is validated through three metallic nanostructures: a nanosphere, a nanorod, and a nanodimer. The results show that the method identifies the intrinsic resonances of each structure, including resonances not excited by a given source and additional resonances arising from the nonlocal response, which are absent in local models. The proposed framework provides physical insight into the modal mechanisms of plasmonic nanostructures and serves as a practical tool for their analysis and design.

</details>

### [180] [Identifying changing partial differential equations using Sampled Local WeakIdent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12479)
*Wenbo Hao,Mengyi Tang,Sung Ha Kang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出SLW-Ident框架，用于从单一给定数据集中识别随空间变化的偏微分方程（PDE）。该方法通过在小块区域采样局部识别方程，并利用残差误差找出方程之间的转换区域，从而准确恢复各方程区域及其对应的控制方程（包括常系数和变系数PDE），即使数据含噪声也能获得准确结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法（如有限元近似表示变化系数）在识别随空间变化控制方程时存在局限，而局部识别方法虽然计算高效且局部识别效果好，但对局部扰动敏感。因此需要一种既能稳定识别过程又能整合全局信息的方法，以从单一数据集中识别变化中的控制方程。

Method: SLW-Ident框架包含以下步骤：(1) 在整个给定区域内采样多个小块（patch）；(2) 对每个采样块独立识别局部方程（基于Local WeakIdent）；(3) 利用各块识别方程之间的残差误差确定不同方程之间的转换边界；(4) 在每个方程区域内，通过统计频次选取最常被识别出的方程作为该区域的控制方程，并能处理常数系数和变化系数PDE。该方法还通过不确定性量化理论建立了主导支撑集选择统计误差与补丁数量之间的关系。

Result: 数值实验表明，SLW-Ident能够准确恢复各方程区域及其控制方程，对于含有变化系数的变化PDE同样有效，并且在给定数据含有噪声的情况下仍能保持准确性。

Conclusion: SLW-Ident通过局部采样与全局信息整合的机制，在保持计算效率的同时克服了纯局部方法的敏感性缺陷，为从单一数据集中识别变化控制方程提供了一种稳健且理论有据的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose Sampled Local WeakIdent (SLW-Ident), a framework for identifying changing governing equations from a single set of given data. Different from a typical approach of using finite element based approximation to represent varying coefficients, this paper explores a local approach in identification of differential equations. First, we present the power of Local WeakIdent which gives good local identification and is also computationally efficient with a small patch size, yet it can be sensitive to local perturbations. We propose SLW-Ident which stabilizes the identification process and also incorporates global information: we first sample patches in the whole given domain, identify equations for each sampled patch, then use residual error of these equations to find the transitions between different equations. We refer to a region where the support of the identified equation does not change to be a region of one equation. Within each region of one equation, we pick the most frequently identified equation as the identified equation, and find constant as well as varying coefficient PDEs within each region of one equation. This is justified by an uncertainty quantification theory that gives the relation between statistical error of dominant support selection and the number of patches. We provide various numerical experiments showing that SLW-Ident accurately recovers the regions of one equation and the governing equations for changing PDEs with varying coefficients even with noisy given data.

</details>


### [181] [BTF-PINN: Enforcing Dirichlet Boundary Conditions Without Boundary Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12823)
*Wenyu Dong,Shuo Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出BTF-PINN（无边界训练物理信息神经网络），一种纯内部求解策略，用于处理齐次Dirichlet边值问题，无需边界训练、边界惩罚或边界一致参数化，通过在损失函数中内嵌本质边界条件实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 齐次Dirichlet边值问题是非插值方法求解Dirichlet问题的核心难点。传统方法通常需要边界训练或边界惩罚项，这些方式在处理不规则几何和高维问题时存在效率与精度瓶颈。

Method: 将本质边界条件嵌入新设计的无边界损失函数中，提出纯内部变分公式，替代传统的边界惩罚方案。理论上证明了该内部公式与原边值问题的等价性，建立了残差权重的尖锐阈值条件，并基于泛函的强制性发展了收敛性分析。

Result: 在高维问题、不规则几何和各向异性椭圆方程上的数值实验验证了BTF-PINN的有效性；与标准边界惩罚PINN的对比表明BTF-PINN在边界迹精度上表现更优。

Conclusion: BTF-PINN通过消除边界训练的依赖，提供了一种更简洁且更精确的齐次Dirichlet问题求解方案，对高维和不规则几何场景具有显著优势。

Abstract: The homogeneous Dirichlet boundary value problem captures the core difficulty of solving Dirichlet problems with non-interpolatory methods. We propose BTF-PINN (Boundary-Training-Free Physics-Informed Neural Network), an interior-only strategy for solving homogeneous Dirichlet boundary value problems that requires no boundary training, boundary penalties, or boundary-conforming parametrizations. The key idea is to embed the essential boundary condition into a newly designed boundary-free loss function. We prove the equivalence between the proposed interior-only variational formulation and the original boundary value problem, establish a sharp threshold condition for the residual weight, and develop a convergence analysis based on the coercivity of the functional. Numerical experiments on high-dimensional problems, irregular geometries, and anisotropic elliptic equations demonstrate the effectiveness of BTF-PINN. Comparisons with standard boundary-penalty PINNs further show that BTF-PINN achieves superior boundary trace accuracy.

</details>


### [182] [Finite element approximation of the linearized Cahn-Hilliard-Cook equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12833)
*Ali Mesforush,Stig Larsson*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性化Cahn-Hilliard-Cook方程的空间有限元离散和时间向后Euler离散，在Wiener过程协方差算子满足适当假设的条件下建立了强收敛估计，结果可视为对非线性Cahn-Hilliard-Cook方程温和解中随机卷积的逼近分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cahn-Hilliard-Cook方程是描述相分离动力学的随机偏微分方程，其数值逼近的严格误差分析尚不完善，特别是涉及随机项（Wiener过程驱动的随机卷积）时的收敛性估计需要系统研究。

Method: 采用标准有限元方法进行空间离散，使用向后Euler方法进行时间离散；基于解析半群的理论框架进行分析，主要工作集中于推导对应确定性Cahn-Hilliard方程的详细误差界，并结合Wiener过程协方差算子的假设推导强收敛估计。

Result: 在Wiener过程协方差算子的适当假设下，证明了空间有限元离散和向后Euler时间离散的强收敛估计，给出了详细的误差界。

Conclusion: 本文建立的误差分析框架和收敛性结果可解释为对非线性Cahn-Hilliard-Cook方程温和解中随机卷积项的逼近结果，为后续非线性情形的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: The linearized Cahn-Hilliard-Cook equation is
  discretized in the spatial variables by a standard finite element
  method. Strong convergence estimates are proved under suitable
  assumptions on the covariance operator of the Wiener process,
  which is driving the equation. The backward Euler time stepping is
  also studied. The analysis is set in a framework based on analytic
  semigroups. The main effort is spent on proving detailed error
  bounds for the corresponding deterministic Cahn-Hilliard
  equation. The results should be interpreted as results on
  approximation of the stochastic convolution, which is a part of
  the mild solution of the nonlinear Cahn-Hilliard-Cook equation.

</details>


### [183] [A robust and optimal method for a sixth-order elliptic singular perturbation problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12886)
*Mingqing Chen,Jianguo Huang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对具有小参数0<ι<1的梯度弹性Kirchhoff板（GEKP）模型的鲁棒非协调有限元方法。该方法在离散空间中使用H^3三次有限元，在双线性形式低阶项和右端项中使用插值映射进入H^2非协调有限元空间。该方法对参数ι具有鲁棒性，无需任何稳定项即可一致地达到h阶收敛，并通过数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度弹性Kirchhoff板模型包含一个小参数ι，这导致标准有限元方法可能在参数趋于0时退化或缺乏鲁棒性。需要设计一种数值方法，使其在参数变化下保持稳定的收敛性能，而无需引入额外的稳定化项。

Method: 采用H^3三次有限元作为离散空间，对双线性形式的低阶项和右端项引入插值映射，将自由度映射到H^2非协调有限元空间。这种"插值到低正则空间"的策略使得低阶项的计算不会破坏方法的鲁棒性，从而无需稳定项即可获得均匀的收敛阶。

Result: 数值方法对参数ι是鲁棒的，在不添加任何稳定项的条件下，关于网格尺寸h具有一致的一阶收敛性。数值实验验证了理论分析的结论。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过合理的插值映射设计，可以在缺乏稳定项的情况下实现对小参数模型的鲁棒且一致收敛的数值求解，为梯度弹性板模型的高效计算提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In this paper we propose a robust nonconforming finite method for a gradient-elastic Kirchhoff plate (GEKP) model with a small parameter $0<ι<1$. While the method employs an $H^3$ cubic finite element for the discrete space, it employs an interpolation mapping into an $H^2$ nonconforming finite element space in both the lower-order terms of the bilinear form and the right-hand side. Notably, the numerical method is robust with respect to the parameter $ι$ and uniformly convergent to order $h$ without any stabilized terms. Finally, some numerical experiments are performed to verify the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [184] [A Counterexample to Robust Second-Order Convergence of the Strang Projector-Splitting Integrator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12893)
*Shiheng Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明经典Strang投影分裂积分器在低秩近似的最小奇异值趋于零时，其全局误差从二阶退化为一阶的间隙是内在的（不可避免的），并构造了一个3×3矩阵微分方程的反例来验证这一结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Strang投影分裂积分器在实际观测中通常收敛到二阶，但现有的误差分析在最小保留奇异值趋于零时只能证明一阶收敛。作者想知道这一阶数与二阶之间的差距是否可以通过更好的分析消除，还是本质性的限制。

Method: 作者构造了3×3矩阵微分方程，满足时间上C^2光滑、矩阵变量光滑、秩二初始数据以及一致有界性、Lipschitz、切向缺陷和正则性等标准假设。通过分析包含四个Strang步骤的一个周期强迫循环，证明精确子流Strang方法在该循环上的局部误差中存在非零的h^2项。该机制的根源是小奇异值相关的因子方向的快速旋转，它保持了第一阶一致性，但变化的投影空间阻止了对称Strang组合中通常期望的抵消。

Result: 作者证明了存在满足所有标准假设的矩阵微分方程，其精确子流Strang方法在一个周期强迫循环（由四个Strang步组成）的局部误差中具有非零的h^2项。重复该循环排除了全局二阶误差界的可能性（该界的常数和步长阈值需独立于保留的奇异值）。因此证明了经典投影分裂方法在这些假设下无法从稳健的一阶结果升级到稳健的二阶。

Conclusion: Strang投影分裂积分器在上述标准假设下，其观测到的二阶收敛与可证明的稳健一阶收敛之间的差距是内在固有的，而非分析技术的不足。因子方向的旋转导致投影空间变化，破坏了对称组合中误差项的抵消，从而使稳健的二阶收敛无法成立。

Abstract: The classical Strang projector-splitting integrator is widely observed to converge with order two, whereas the error analysis that remains uniform as the smallest singular value retained in the low-rank approximation tends to zero proves only order one. We show that this gap is intrinsic under the standard assumptions. We construct $3\times3$ matrix differential equations that are $C^2$ in time and smooth in the matrix variable, with rank-two initial data that satisfy uniform boundedness, Lipschitz, tangency-defect, and regularity bounds. Nevertheless, the exact-subflow Strang method has a nonzero $h^2$ term in the local error over one periodic forcing cycle consisting of four Strang steps. Repetition of that cycle rules out a global second-order bound whose constant and stepsize threshold are independent of the retained singular values. The mechanism is a rapid rotation of the factor directions associated with the small singular value: first-order consistency is preserved, but the changing projection spaces prevent the cancellation normally expected from a symmetric Strang composition. Hence the robust first-order result cannot, under these assumptions alone, be upgraded to robust second order for the classical projector-splitting method.

</details>


### [185] [Analysis of Error Propagation in Autoencoder-Based Reduced-Order Neural Ordinary Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13132)
*Jingyi Zhang,Gwanghyun Jo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文通过路径积分恒等式分析神经ODE降阶模型的长时程外推误差，将局部差异注入与潜动力放大分离，并利用多步Jacobian范数量化传输敏感性，在Burgers和Gray-Scott系统上揭示了两种截然不同的误差演化模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经ODE降阶模型在局部预测上精度相近，但长时程外推行为可能存在显著差异，需要一种理论工具来理解这种差异的来源与机制。

Method: 开发了一个路径积分恒等式将局部误差注入与潜动力中的误差放大分离；引入多步Jacobian范数来量化传输敏感性，从而区分不同的误差传播机制。

Result: 在Burgers系统中，预测误差保持有界，主要与持续的局部误差有关；在Gray-Scott系统中，外推过程中误差显著放大，Jacobian范数充当敏感性诊断工具而非物理预测精度的直接指标。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效区分不同动力学系统外推误差的传播模式，Jacobian范数在两类系统中扮演不同角色，为神经ODE降阶模型的长期预测可靠性提供了诊断框架。

Abstract: Neural ODE reduced-order models often achieve comparable local prediction accuracy, yet their long-horizon extrapolation behavior can differ substantially. To analyze this discrepancy, we develop a path-integral identity that separates local discrepancy injection from amplification in the learned latent dynamics. The associated multi-step Jacobian norms quantify transport sensitivity and distinguish different propagation regimes. Experiments on the Burgers and Gray--Scott systems exhibit two distinct patterns of error evolution. In Burgers systems, prediction errors remain bounded and are primarily associated with persistent local discrepancies. In contrast, Gray--Scott systems exhibit pronounced amplification during extrapolation, where Jacobian norms serve as sensitivity diagnostics rather than direct indicators of physical prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [186] [Performance Evaluation of an Adaptive Quadrature and a Double Exponential Formula Using Arbitrary-Precision Floating-Point Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13187)
*Tomonori Kouya*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文使用GNU MPFR库实现了AQE11D（基于Ninomiya自适应9点Newton-Cotes法则扩展的高阶规则序列）和Takahasi-Mori双指数（DE）公式，并在Kahaner的21个测试问题上评估两者。在绝对容差10^-50和10^-100下，AQE11D在所有21个问题上均达到目标精度，但在强端点奇异性（如1/√x）下需要约5.4×10^7次函数求值，是DE公式的约7×10^4倍。DE公式在两个容差下于18个问题上收敛，展示了其应对端点奇异性的优势，但在积分区间内部存在奇异性时会失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究任意精度数值积分方法在极高精度要求下的性能表现，比较自适应Newton-Cotes扩展规则（AQE11D）与双指数（DE）公式在包含端点奇异性和内部奇异性测试问题上的收敛能力和效率差异。

Method: 利用GNU MPFR库的任意精度浮点运算，实现了AQE11D（Ninomiya自适应9点Newton-Cotes法则扩展高阶规则序列）和Takahasi-Mori双指数（DE）公式，在Kahaner的21个标准测试问题上，分别在10^-50和10^-100两个绝对容差下进行数值评估与比较。

Result: AQE11D在两个容差下均能在全部21个测试问题上达到目标精度；但对于强端点奇异性（如1/√x），在10^-100容差下需要约5.4×10^7次函数求值，约为DE公式的7×10^4倍。DE公式在两个容差下对18个问题收敛，但无法处理积分区间内部存在奇异性的问题。

Conclusion: AQE11D具有普适的收敛性但效率低；DE公式对端点奇异性高效但对内部奇异性失效，二者在任意精度数值积分场景中各有优劣，互为补充。

Abstract: Using arbitrary-precision arithmetic provided by the GNU Multiple Precision Floating-Point Reliable Library, we implement AQE11D---that is, Ninomiya's adaptive 9-point Newton--Cotes rule extended with a sequence of higher-order rules---and Takahasi and Moris' double exponential (DE) formula. We evaluate them for Kahaner's 21 test problems. For both absolute tolerances $10^{-50}$ and $10^{-100}$, AQE11D attains target accuracy on all 21 problems; however, for strong endpoint singularity such as $1/\sqrt{x}$, it requires about $5.4\times10^{7}$ function evaluations at $10^{-100}$, roughly $7\times10^{4}$ times as many as the DE formula. The formula converges on 18 problems at both tolerances, demonstrating its strength against endpoint singularities but also its failure, as it stands, on problems with a singularity inside the integration interval.

</details>


### [187] [Stochastic resistive Hall--MHD with current fluctuations: martingale weak solutions via a convergent structure-preserving finite element method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13407)
*Agus L. Soenjaya*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文研究了有界凸多面体区域上的随机电阻性Hall-MHD系统，采用兼容离散de Rham复形基的完全离散、线性隐式、结构保持混合有限元方法，精确保持磁Gauss定律，并证明了离散解的子列收敛到有限能量弱鞅解，从而构造性地证明了带curl型噪声的随机Hall-MHD系统解的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机Hall-MHD系统在等离子体物理中具有重要应用，但其数值分析和解的存在性研究仍具挑战性，尤其是在有界凸多面体区域上结合非线性完全导电边界条件和curl型噪声时。现有方法难以同时保证磁场散度约束的精确保持和随机系统的收敛性分析。

Method: 采用基于兼容离散de Rham复形的混合有限元方法进行空间离散，结合线性隐式格式进行时间离散；对动量方程施加乘性Gaussian噪声，对感应方程引入由广义Ohm定律中电阻和Hall部分的随机扰动产生的结构curl型噪声；利用鞅理论（子列收敛）分析离散解的极限行为。

Result: 证明了离散解的子列收敛到有限能量弱鞅解，从而给出随机Hall-MHD系统（含curl型噪声）的构造性存在性证明；数值实验展示了随机动力学特性、磁场散度约束的精确保持以及方法的收敛性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为带curl型噪声的随机Hall-MHD系统提供了构造性的存在性结果，同时提出了兼具结构保持性（精确保持Gauss磁定律）和计算效率（线性隐式）的数值格式，为后续相关研究奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We study the stochastic resistive Hall--magnetohydrodynamic (Hall--MHD) system on bounded convex polyhedral domains, subject to nonlinear perfectly conducting boundary conditions. The system is driven by multiplicative Gaussian forcing in the momentum equation together with structured curl-type noise in the induction equation arising from stochastic perturbations of the resistive and Hall parts in the generalised Ohm law. We develop a fully discrete, linearly implicit, structure-preserving mixed finite element method based on a compatible discrete de Rham complex, which preserves the magnetic Gauss law exactly. We prove subsequential convergence of the discrete solutions to a finite-energy weak martingale solution, thereby obtaining a constructive existence result for the stochastic Hall--MHD system with curl-type noise. Numerical experiments illustrate the stochastic dynamics, exact divergence preservation, and convergence of the method.

</details>

### [188] [Intermediate scattering function of Brownian particles in a tilted cosine potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12421)
*Regina Rusch,Thomas Franosch*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文求解了倾斜余弦势中布朗粒子的Fokker-Planck方程，推导出包含两个波矢的广义中间散射函数（ISF），用以捕捉周期势中系统的完整时空动力学。利用Bloch定理和谱理论框架数值计算本征函数与本征值，并通过含时微扰理论展开ISF，推导出低阶矩（均方位移、含时扩散系数、偏度、非高斯参数）。分析结果经布朗动力学模拟验证，并针对不同倾斜力区间与谐振近似和确定性极限进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在周期势（如倾斜余弦势）中运动的布朗粒子是理解许多物理系统（如超离子导体、胶体扩散、分子马达等）的基础模型。现有工作通常只关注均方位移或扩散系数等单一标量量，缺乏能完整描述系统时空动力学的高阶关联函数。本文旨在通过广义中间散射函数提供对系统完整时空动力学的解析与数值描述，填补这一空白。

Method: 1. 建立一维过阻尼布朗粒子在倾斜余弦势中的Langevin/Fokker-Planck方程；2. 利用势场的周期性，通过Bloch定理将问题纳入谱理论框架，数值计算本征函数与本征值；3. 推导包含两个波矢的广义ISF；4. 采用含时微扰理论对ISF进行展开，推导各低阶矩（MSD、含时扩散系数、偏度、非高斯参数）；5. 通过布朗动力学模拟验证解析结果，并与谐振近似及确定性极限进行对比。

Result: 论文成功推导并数值计算了倾斜余弦势中布朗粒子的广义ISF，获得其对应的概率密度。含时微扰理论给出了低阶矩的解析表达式，包括均方位移、含时扩散系数、偏度与非高斯参数。所有解析结果均通过布朗动力学模拟得到验证，并在不同倾斜力强度区间下与谐振近似和确定性极限进行了系统比较。

Conclusion: 该工作为倾斜周期势中布朗运动的时空关联提供了完整的分析框架，广义ISF作为统一描述工具可以涵盖从扩散到输运再到确定性运动的多个动力学状态。该框架可推广至更高维或多粒子系统，为研究周期势中输运现象提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We solve the Fokker-Planck equation for a Brownian particle in a tilted cosine potential and derive the intermediate scattering function (ISF), which captures the full spatio-temporal dynamics of the system. The model consists of a single overdamped Brownian particle in one dimension. We derive a generalized ISF comprising two wave vectors to describe correlations in the periodic potential. Exploiting the periodicity via Bloch's theorem, we formulate the problem within a spectral-theoretical framework and numerically compute the corresponding eigenfunctions and eigenvalues, from which we obtain the ISF and the probability density. Using time-dependent perturbation theory, we expand the ISF and derive low-order moments, including the mean-square displacement, time-dependent diffusivity, skewness, and the non-Gaussian parameter. Our analytical results are validated by Brownian-dynamics simulations and analyzed focussing on different regimes of the tilting force. The results are compared to a harmonic approximation and the deterministic limit.

</details>


### [189] [Thermodynamics of Learning: A Typed Four-Component Accounting of Memory, Fit, and Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12791)
*Akihito Sudo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文为有限状态学习设备建立了一个类型化会计框架，将"设备已记录的"与"对未来任务有价值的"分离为四个独立分量：训练侧拟合泛函Φ_fit、记录相关性股票J_D、更新侧搜索分类账σ_M和操作资本价值V(M;T,b)。论文给出三条主要结果：(I)分离定理——存在设备族使记录相关性和世界相关性增长n ln2而资本增益恰为零；(II)资本化账本——在flat*机制下给出资本化效率η_cap≤1的精确上界及等号充要条件；(III)价值保持——给出保留缺口的双层对齐域，包含精确汇率与原始记录股票汇率，边界由一次性密码证人标记。该框架处理的是有限设备在任务分布偏移下的价值保持，而非统计泛化理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机在于区分"学习设备已经记录了什么"和"未来任务中什么才真正有价值"这两个不同的量。现有的学习理论框架往往将数据拟合与泛化能力混为一谈，而本文希望在有限状态学习设备的语境下，建立一种精确的会计机制来分离训练侧拟合、记录相关性、搜索过程以及最终的操作价值，从而揭示在任务分布偏移条件下，设备的"账面记录"与其"真实资本价值"之间可能存在的巨大鸿沟。

Method: 本文采用类型化会计（typed accounting）方法，将有限状态学习设备的四个分量形式化：训练侧拟合泛函Φ_fit、记录-相关性股票J_D=I(M;D)、更新侧搜索分类账σ_M、以及操作资本价值V(M;T,b)（定义为有信息协议类与删除内存读取端口后的盲类之间的工作缺口）。在flat*机制下推导出精确的flat*提取恒等式和普适分类账恒等式，结合(F5')稳定性和无丢弃记录相关性条件(f)，建立资本化效率的不等式界，并通过构造一次性密码证人来标记价值保留的边界。

Result: 主要结果包括：(I) 分离定理：对每个n存在设备族使记录相关性和世界相关性增长n ln2而资本增益为零，说明高记录相关性并不必然带来操作价值；(II) 在flat*机制下，数据无关的更新永远不增加V；并且对于(F5')稳定的M局部更新，在条件(f)下资本化效率η_cap≤1，同时给出等号的充分必要条件；(III) 价值保留方面：保留缺口L_gen无符号约束，保留比率ρ_gen不限制在[0,1]内，给出两层对齐域——侧信息调整的记录拟合与价值之间的精确汇率，以及在联合侧信息中立条件下原始记录股票的汇率，边界由显式一次性密码证人标记。

Conclusion: 结论表明，有限学习设备的记录相关性（账面价值）与操作资本价值（实际价值）是原则上不同的量，可以完全分离。在flat*机制下数据无关更新不会提升操作价值。论文建立了有限设备在任务分布偏移下价值保留的精确条件与边界，但这些是关于有限设备的会计学陈述，不构成统计泛化理论。该框架为理解"设备记住了什么"与"设备能做什么"之间的差异提供了严格的数学语言。

Abstract: What a finite learning device has recorded and what will hold value for it on future tasks are not the same quantity. We develop a typed accounting for finite-state learning devices that separates four components: a training-side fit functional $Φ_{\mathrm{fit}}$, the record-correlation stock $J_{D}=I(M;D)$, an update-side search ledger $σ_{M}$, and an operational capital value $V(M;T,b)$. This value is the work gap between an informed protocol class and a blind class obtained by deleting the memory-read port and re-optimizing from scratch. (I) Separation: for every $n$, there is a device family on which record correlation and world correlation grow by $n\ln 2$ while the capital gain is exactly zero. In the $\mathrm{flat}^{*}$ regime, data-free updates never increase $V$. (II) Capitalization ledger: an exact $\mathrm{flat}^{*}$ extraction identity and a universal ledger identity give, for (F5$'$)-stable $M$-local updates under a no-discarded-record-correlation condition (f), the bound $η_{\mathrm{cap}}\le 1$ for the capitalization efficiency $η_{\mathrm{cap}}=ΔV/(k T\,σ_{M})$, together with necessary and sufficient conditions for equality. (III) Value retention: for the retention gap $L_{\mathrm{gen}}$ and retention ratio $ρ_{\mathrm{gen}}$ (the former carries no sign constraint; the latter is defined for positive training-side value and is not confined to $[0,1]$) we give a two-layer alignment domain: an exact exchange rate between value and the side-information-adjusted record fit $I(M';D\mid Y)$ without any record-side-information independence assumption, and a raw record-stock exchange rate under a joint side-information neutrality condition $(M,D)\perp Y$, whose boundary is marked by an explicit one-time-pad witness. These are statements about finite-device value retention under task-distribution shift, not a theory of statistical generalization.

</details>


### [190] [Local and quasilocal conservation laws of three-state IRF cellular automata and their quantum deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13080)
*Tomaž Prosen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用贴片矩阵乘积方法研究了两类可逆三态交互环绕面元胞自动机（物种保持与物种翻转规则）及其相干量子变形。研究发现这两类经典规则共享一族含辅助维数为三的拟局域守恒律，其欧拉速度的下界为±√(3/23)，与Klobas和Prosen的数值预测一致。物种保持规则还额外拥有无限塔状严格局域守恒荷，量子变形则产生低键维不变态（疤痕候选）、指数长寿拟局域准模和对角拟局域电荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解可积或近可积经典-量子系统的守恒量与动力学性质，特别是验证Klobas和Prosen提出的交互环绕面元胞自动机中声活动荷的完备性，并通过量子变形研究相干结构的普适性。

Method: 采用贴片矩阵乘积方法（patch-matrix-product methods），在显式平移不变ansatz框架下构造拟局域守恒量族，分析其欧拉速度谱；对量子变形采用相同的代数结构构造不变态、准模和Mazur界的对角电荷。

Result: 两类经典规则共享带辅助维数三的单参数拟局域守恒律族；非零欧拉速度仅为±√(3/23)，给出了更大守恒扇区最大欧拉速度的严格下界；物种翻转规则的结果与Klobas-Prosen报告的数值0.361吻合；物种保持规则额外有无穷严格局域电荷塔；量子变形产生疤痕候选态、指数长寿准模和非零Mazur界。

Conclusion: 结果表明该系统中已识别的声活动荷是完备的（物种翻转情形），√(3/23)是最大欧拉速度的严格下界；量子变形保留了经典规则的代数结构并产生丰富的动力学现象（疤痕物理、长寿命准模、非零Mazur界），表明该家族具有统一的经典-量子可解结构。

Abstract: Using patch-matrix-product methods, we study two reversible three-state interaction-round-a-face cellular automata introduced by Klobas and Prosen [J. Phys. A 55, 094003 (2022)] - the species-preserving and species-flipping rules - and a coherent quantum deformation interpolating between them. Within an explicit translationally invariant ansatz, the two classical rules share a one-parameter family of quasi-local conservation laws with an auxiliary-dimension-three realization. Every regular member is also the first centered logarithmic derivative of an analytic auxiliary-dimension-two family passing through the identity observable. On the closed span of this family and the three elementary local charges, the only nonzero Euler velocities are $\pm\sqrt{3/23}$. Consequently, $\sqrt{3/23}$ is a rigorous lower bound on the maximal Euler speed in any larger conserved sector. For the species-flipping rule, this value agrees with the reported extrapolated value $0.361$ of Klobas and Prosen, strongly supporting completeness of the known sound-active charges. The species-preserving rule admits, in addition, a staggered generating family whose logarithmic derivatives form an infinite tower of strictly local charges. In the quantum deformation, the same algebraic structures yield exact low-bond-dimension invariant states (scar candidates), exponentially long-lived quasi-local quasimodes, and diagonal quasi-local charges that produce a nonzero Mazur bound after projection away from the three elementary local charges.

</details>


### [191] [Equivariant learning of a transferable three-dimensional classical density functional](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13506)
*Bingqing Cheng*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种新方法：直接从三维平衡密度场中学习经典密度泛函理论（DFT）的过量自由能泛函，无需自由能或化学势标签。学习得到的单一泛函可在不同温度、系统尺寸和统计系综间迁移，能复现结构因子、状态方程、液-汽共存和界面变宽等未作为训练目标的性质，并能预测复杂三维几何（如胶体间溶剂耗尽桥、螺旋孔吸附）中的非单调力与吸附行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液体的集体行为对热力学条件、界面和限域十分敏感，预测每种新状态通常需要单独的原子模拟，成本高昂。经典密度泛函理论提供可复用的变分框架，但其核心的过量自由能泛函一般未知，已有学习近似多局限于平面或低维情形。因此，如何在无需标签的前提下从三维平衡数据中学习一个具有空间对称性和变分一致性的普适泛函，是核心动机。

Method: 作者提出直接从完全三维的平衡密度场学习过量自由能泛函。方法的关键在于：1）仅使用密度场数据作为输入，无需自由能或化学势标签；2）在构建泛函时显式保持空间对称性（平移与旋转不变性）以及变分一致性（即泛函导数与化学势关系正确）；3）训练得到的单一泛函被用于跨温度、系统尺寸和统计系综的迁移测试。应用层面，该方法被用于预测复杂三维几何如溶剂耗尽桥和螺旋孔道中的吸附现象。

Result: 单一学习到的泛函能成功迁移至不同温度、系统尺寸和统计系综，并准确复现结构因子、状态方程、液-汽共存曲线和界面变宽等性质——这些均未作为训练目标。在复杂三维几何中，该方法预测了胶体之间溶剂耗尽桥形成与断裂所伴随的非单调力，以及互连螺旋孔道中的吸附行为，表明学习到的泛函具备预测性泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作证明平衡密度数据可被转化为一个可迁移的热力学生成器，将微观液体结构与响应、相行为和集体现象联系起来。所提出的方法为三维液体的通用DFT泛函学习开辟了道路，有望大幅减少针对不同热力学状态开展繁琐原子模拟的需求。

Abstract: Liquids exhibit collective behavior that depends sensitively on thermodynamic conditions, interfaces and confinement, yet predicting each new state commonly requires a separate atomistic simulation. Classical density functional theory offers a reusable variational description, but its central excess free-energy functional is generally unknown, and learned approximations have largely remained restricted to planar or lower-dimensional settings. Here we show that this functional can be learned directly from fully three-dimensional equilibrium density fields while preserving spatial symmetry and variational consistency, without free-energy or chemical-potential labels. A single learned functional transfers across temperatures, system sizes and statistical ensembles, and recovers structure factors, the equation of state, liquid--vapor coexistence and interfacial broadening, none of which are used as training targets. Applied to complex three-dimensional geometries, it predicts the non-monotonic force associated with formation and rupture of a solvent-depleted bridge between colloids and adsorption in an interconnected gyroid pore. These results demonstrate that equilibrium density data can be converted into a transferable thermodynamic generator connecting microscopic liquid structure to response, phase behavior and collective phenomena.

</details>
